Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing feed-forward methods predict 3D Gaussians pixel-wise for each frame, suffering from duplicated Gaussians and view-dependent biases that hinder effective learning of scene motion. We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.

</details>

Result: 在5-shot设定下，该方法在多个非典型领域上相比现有SOTA L2I方法在视觉保真度和对齐度上均取得一致改进。

Abstract: The layout-to-image (L2I) task enables fine-grained control over image generation via object categories and spatial layouts. However, existing L2I methods yield fragmented and distorted generations under few-shot atypical settings. We term this failure as representation fragmentation, arising from a granularity mismatch that entangles semantic identity with visual details. To address this issue, we propose a representation-driven framework that disentangles semantics from primitives for robust few-shot adaptation. Specifically, Semantic Anchoring aggregates categorical semantics into anchors for stable identity, while Primitive Imbuing models recomposable primitives for robust local detail modeling. Conceptual Steering further regulates optimization with a saliency-aware objective to preserve foreground semantic consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in the 5-shot regime over state-of-the-art L2I methods in both visual fidelity and alignment across diverse atypical domains. The source code is publicly available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时流式视频到视频编辑（V2V）在直播、游戏等交互式应用中至关重要，但现有方法受限于时序一致性和推理吞吐量，难以在消费级 GPU 上实现高分辨率实时编辑。

Method: (1) Hybrid DiT 架构：在部分块中引入 softmax attention 以增强局部建模能力，同时保留线性层的效率；(2) Cycle-Reverse Regularization：通过 flow matching 从生成内容预测源帧，强制语义一致性，无需成对长视频即可提升时序一致性；(3) 高效系统协同设计：融合 GDN 核与混合精度量化（MPQ），针对 NVIDIA Blackwell（RTX 5090）架构优化 Tensor Core 利用率。

Result: 在单张 RTX 5090 上实现 1280×704 分辨率、24 FPS 端到端实时视频编辑，DiT 核心达 58 FPS。在时序一致性和系统吞吐量上显著优于现有 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: SANA-Streaming 通过系统-算法协同设计，首次在消费级 GPU 上实现了高分辨率实时流式视频编辑，为交互式视频编辑应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究实时流式视频到视频编辑（V2V），属于多模态生成方向下的视频编辑任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's high-weight continuous multimodal and video generation priorities via hybrid DiT and flow matching, but subtopic is editing rather than core generation. Signals: Hybrid Diffusion Transformer, flow matching cycle-reverse regularization, real-time video editing on consumer GPU, NVIDIA Blackwell system co-design, high-resolution 1280x704 at 24 FPS

Abstract: Real-time streaming video-to-video editing (V2V) is critical for interactive applications such as live broadcasting and gaming, yet it remains a formidable challenge due to the stringent requirements for temporal consistency and inference throughput. In this paper, we present SANA-Streaming, a system-algorithm co-designed framework for high-resolution, real-time streaming video editing on consumer GPUs, with the following three core designs: (1) Hybrid Diffusion Transformer architecture introduces softmax attention in part of the blocks to improve local modeling capabilities while preserving the efficiency of linear layers. (2) Cycle-Reverse Regularization is a novel training strategy that enforces semantic consistency by predicting source frames from generated content via flow matching, improving temporal consistency without requiring paired long edited videos. (3) Efficient System Co-design combines fused GDN kernels and Mixed-Precision Quantization (MPQ) optimized for the NVIDIA Blackwell (RTX 5090) architecture. By profiling real-world throughput, our MPQ maximizes Tensor Core utilization while maintaining generation quality. The resulting system achieves real-time 1280 x 704 resolution editing at 24 end-to-end FPS on a single RTX 5090 GPU, with the DiT core running at 58 FPS. Experimental results demonstrate that our co-design approach significantly outperforms existing SOTA methods in both temporal coherence and system throughput.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在修复任务中受限于标准分类器自由引导（CFG）中条件分支与无条件分支的强耦合，导致扭曲内容被复制而非修复。作者希望在不重新训练模型的前提下，利用扩散先验增强低分辨率和扭曲视频的结构保真度与时间一致性。

Method: 提出 Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG)，在采样过程中将无条件分支评估于更干净的扩散时间步，提供"前瞻先验"以保留几何结构并抑制扭曲内容复制。该时间偏差在采样过程中逐步退火，使模型从结构校正过渡到细节精修。DTG 可与任意现成的修复模块即插即用。

Result: 在 AI 生成和真实世界视频上均实现了结构保真度和时间稳定性的显著提升。构建了 GenWarp480 基准（4400 个 480p 扭曲视频），专注于生成式退化（扭曲人脸、身体错位、空间伪影），实验表明方法无需训练即可大幅改善修复质量。

Conclusion: DTG-Restore 是一种有效的无训练视频修复框架，通过时间步解耦的引导策略利用扩散先验，在无需重新训练的情况下提升了视频超分辨率与修复的性能，具有良好的即插即用性和泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出无训练的视频超分辨率与修复框架，属于视频质量增强与修复范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的视频编辑/修复方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Training-free video super-resolution is useful but not aligned with user's priority on autoregressive/diffusion video generation or continuous multimodal models. Signals: video super-resolution, training-free, diffusion refinement, decoupled time guidance, generative video restoration

Abstract: Recent progress in video diffusion models has enabled remarkable generative fidelity, yet leveraging these priors for restoration remains limited by the strong coupling between conditional and unconditional branches in standard classifier-free guidance. We introduce a training-free framework that enhances distorted and low-resolution videos by decoupling these signals in time. Our proposed Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG) evaluates the unconditional branch at a cleaner diffusion timestep, providing a lookahead prior that preserves geometry while suppressing replication of warped content. This temporal bias is annealed throughout sampling, allowing the model to transition from structure correction to detail refinement without retraining. Combined with any off-the-shelf restoration module in a plug-and-play manner, our approach improves perceptual coherence and restores plausible structure in AIgenerated and real-world videos alike. To facilitate evaluation, we curate GenWarp480, a benchmark of 4,400 distorted 480p videos synthesized from diverse text-to-video models. GenWarp480 focuses on characteristic generative degradations such as warped faces, body misalignments, and spatial artifacts, providing a purpose-built testbed for assessing robustness to generative errors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves significant improvements in structural fidelity and temporal stability without any model training.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI图像编辑器（如Nano Banana）能通过文本提示生成视觉上令人满意的编辑结果，但其生成性质常引入空间错位、纹理失真和内容幻觉，这些对需要像素级保真度的下游工作流是有害的。现有方法缺乏在保持结构忠实性的同时保留GenAI感知增强效果的手段。

Method: 提出"结构保持GenAI融合"问题设定，设计后处理框架：首先建立输入图像与GenAI增强图像之间的粗粒度空间和光度对应关系，然后执行融合阶段，将期望的增强效果转移过来，同时抑制幻觉内容。框架作为黑盒后处理，不依赖GenAI编辑器的内部结构。

Result: 与光真实感风格迁移和图像融合的代表性方法相比，该方法在保持像素级结构一致性和输入分辨率的同时，更好地保留了美学质量。

Conclusion: 所提出的后处理融合框架能有效解决GenAI图像编辑中的结构忠实性问题，在感知增强和结构保真之间取得良好平衡，且适用于黑盒GenAI编辑器。

Classification reason: 该论文研究GenAI图像编辑中的结构保持融合问题，属于图像编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses a practical post-processing issue for GenAI image editing but is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: GenAI image editing, structural refinement, content hallucination, post-processing fusion, not autoregressive or continuous model

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) image editors, such as Nano Banana, produce visually compelling results for retouching tasks, enabling non-experts to edit images through text prompts alone. However, the generative nature of these models often introduces spatial misalignment, texture distortion, and content hallucination, all of which are detrimental to downstream workflows that require pixel-level fidelity. We identify a problem setting we call "structure-preserving GenAI fusion" for black-box GenAI image retouching: retain the perceptual enhancements of a GenAI output while enforcing structural faithfulness to the original input image. To address this problem, we propose a post-processing framework that fuses an input image with its GenAI-enhanced counterpart by first establishing coarse spatial and photometric correspondences, then performing a fusion stage that transfers desired enhancements while suppressing hallucinated content. In the absence of direct prior work in this setting, we evaluate our framework against representative methods from photorealistic style transfer and image fusion. Our experiments demonstrate that our method better preserves aesthetic quality while maintaining pixel-level structural consistency and the input resolution.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型生成的物理示意图虽然视觉上合理，但系统性地存在力向量幻觉、违反守恒定律和几何约束等问题，无法满足物理教学和科学交流中对严格物理准确性的需求。

Method: PhyDrawGen 采用三阶段神经符号管道：(1) LLM 从问题文本中提取类型化场景图；(2) 确定性求解器将场景图转换为平面直线图（PSLG），编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑为精确几何图元；(3) 微调后的 Qwen-VL 模型执行视觉接地提议-验证循环，迭代修正任何约束违反。

Result: 在包含 1,449 个问题的基准上（涵盖力学、光学和电磁学），PhyDrawGen 在物理准确性上显著优于 GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Gemini 3 Pro，在非常规物体问题上也表现出鲁棒的物理准确性。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法结合 LLM 的场景理解与确定性物理求解器，能够有效解决生成模型在物理示意图生成中的幻觉和约束违反问题，为科学可视化提供了可靠方案。

Classification reason: 该论文从自然语言生成物理示意图，涉及对生成结果的迭代修正与约束满足，属于可控生成/编辑范畴。最接近的规范子主题是"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing），因为其核心是生成并迭代修正物理示意图，可视为一种受物理约束的视觉生成/编辑任务。同时该论文也涉及多模态理解（LLM提取场景图）与生成（Qwen-VL生成图像），因此也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on physics diagram generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Neuro-symbolic pipeline for physics diagrams, Outperforms GPT-5-image on physical accuracy, Uses LLM, deterministic solver, and fine-tuned VLM, No direct relevance to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>

Conclusion: 为3DGS场景的物体移除与Inpainting提供了系统的基准比较，揭示了2D Inpainter选择与场景初始化策略对3D一致性的关键影响，并贡献了新的评测数据集。

Abstract: The tasks of object removal and inpainting 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes face challenges such as 3D consistency across camera views. In comparing 2D inpainters and their suitability for the 3D domain, we find that reconstruction-based inpainters outperform generative diffusion models in 3D consistency. Integrating these 2D inpainters into different single-step methods for creating and finetuning 3DGS scenes, our results indicate that initializing the scene from scratch produces higher quality results than finetuning the existing scene. Using a state-of-the-art generative 2D inpainter, we create a straightforward baseline to underline the importance of object removal before inpainting in the 3D setting. Since 360° datasets rarely include real-world ground truths, and challenging occlusion scenarios are equally sparse, we introduce a novel multi-object scene with recorded ground truth data and many views with object occlusions.

Method: 提出一种推理时机制：首先提取退化概念向量（degradation concept vectors），然后结合瓶颈修补（bottleneck patching）与无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance），在采样过程中引导模型远离退化流形，从而生成一致改善的图像，无需任何模型重训练。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在无条件扩散模型上成功实现了全局低层感知编辑，在美学质量提升方面优于现有的 h-space patching 基线，且无需额外训练数据或模型微调。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过退化概念向量与瓶颈修补结合无分类器引导，可以在无条件扩散模型中实现有效的训练无关图像编辑，为低层感知编辑提供了新的通用框架。

Signals: Unconditional diffusion, low-level perceptual editing, training-free inference, bottleneck patching, classifier-free guidance

Abstract: Unconditional diffusion models offer powerful generative priors, yet steering them toward aesthetically enhanced outputs remains largely unexplored. We show that h-space patching, the dominant paradigm for training-free diffusion editing, systematically fails for global, low-level transformations required for aesthetic and perceptual refinement. We introduce a novel, generalized framework for image-editing in unconditional diffusion models without explicit training. This inference-time mechanism operates on low-level features by extracting degradation concept vectors and combining bottleneck patching with classifier-free guidance to guide sampling away from the degraded manifold, producing consistently improved images without any model retraining.

</details>

TL;DR: Dex2HOI 是一个统一的扩散模型，用于从文本生成单手/双手与单物体/双物体的人-物交互动作序列。它采用双流扩散架构，每个物体有独立交互流并通过双向交叉注意力协调，结合运动融合网络、手部相对物体表示和接触感知条件，通过自回归前缀条件窗口采样实现任意长度序列的实时生成，推理速度相比先前方法提升最高540倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D人-物交互（HOI）生成研究主要关注单物体操作，忽略了人类自然协调双手同时操作多个物体的能力。作者旨在填补这一空白，实现更真实、更具表现力的多物体双手交互动作生成。

Method: 提出Dex2HOI，核心是双流扩散（Dual-Stream Diffusion）架构：每个物体有独立的交互流，通过双向交叉注意力协调两个流的信息。引入运动融合网络（Motion Fusion Network）结合手部相对物体表示和全序列接触感知条件。通过自回归前缀条件窗口采样扩散过程，实现任意长度序列的实时生成，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在单物体和双物体基准上均取得最先进的定量结果，推理速度相比先前方法提升最高540倍（x540），达到实时生成速度。

Conclusion: Dex2HOI首次实现了从文本到双手双物体交互动作的统一生成，超越了传统单物体HOI生成范式，向更具表现力的多物体操作迈出了重要一步。

Classification reason: 该论文研究双手双物体的人-物交互（HOI）动作生成，属于人体动作生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D Human-Object Interaction (HOI) generation have enabled increasingly realistic motion synthesis, particularly for single-object manipulation. Yet current research overlooks an inherent property of human behavior: people naturally coordinate both hands and manipulate multiple objects simultaneously. To address this gap, we present Dex2HOI, a unified diffusion model for single- and two-object HOI synthesis from text. At its core, Dex2HOI employs a Dual-Stream Diffusion approach, where each object is processed in a dedicated interaction stream and coordinated through bidirectional cross-attention. To synthesize the final motion, we introduce a Motion Fusion Network integrated with novel hand-relative object representations and contact-aware conditioning applied across the whole sequence. By sampling the diffusion process autoregressively over prefix-conditioned windows, Dex2HOI generates arbitrarily long sequences at real-time speed omitting redundant test-time optimization, achieving up to x540 inference speed-up over prior state-of-the-art methods. Extensive evaluation on both single- and two-object benchmarks demonstrates state-of-the-art quantitative results, marking a step beyond conventional single-object HOI generation and toward expressive multi-object manipulation. Code and models will be released upon acceptance.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出 OmniME 框架，包含三个互补组件：(1) 回顾性特征监督（Retrospective Feature Supervision）——在 Transformer 层间施加从粗到细的一致性约束；(2) 运动保持机制（Motion Preservation Mechanism）——根据源-目标相似性关注细微变化；(3) 三元组语义对齐（Triplet-based Semantic Alignment）——增强文本-运动对应关系。三者形成统一的监督范式来平衡变化与不变。

Result: 在 MotionFix 和 STANCE Adjustment 数据集上的大量实验表明，OmniME 在编辑对齐方面达到了最先进的性能，验证了统一学习框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 全监督正负学习框架能够有效平衡运动编辑中的变化与不变性，三个互补组件的协同作用优于现有启发式方法，为文本驱动的运动编辑提供了新的统一范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于文本的人体运动编辑（text-based human motion editing），属于人体动作生成与编辑范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on motion editing, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: motion editing, human motion, diffusion-based, positive-negative learning, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Text-based human motion editing aims to modify existing motion sequences according to natural language instructions while maintaining the consistency of the original motion. Existing diffusion-based approaches often rely on heuristic similarity cues or coarse global conditioning, leading to motion distortion and suboptimal semantic alignment. The key challenge lies in balancing change (i.e. precisely editing target regions) and invariance (i.e. preserving unedited parts). To handle such challenge, we propose an Omni-Supervised Positive-Negative Learning framework, named OmniME. Our method integrates three complementary components: (1) retrospective feature supervision that enforces coarse-to-fine consistency across transformer layers,(2) motion preservation mechanism that focuses on subtle variations according to the source-target similarity, and (3) triplet-based semantic alignment that strengthens text-motion correspondence. Together, these components form a unified supervision paradigm that balances change and invariance. Extensive experiments on the MotionFix and STANCE Adjustment datasets demonstrate that OmniME achieves state-of-the-art performance in editing alignment, validating the effectiveness of our unified learning framework. Our source codes and models have been released at:

Result: 消融实验验证了块处理在延迟与质量间的权衡效果；实时性能在延迟上与先前方案相当；感知研究显示 TokTalk 在 3D 面部表现的质量、表现力和可控性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TokTalk 证明了直接从 Audio-LLM 音频 token 生成富有表现力的实时 3D 面部动画的可行性，为构建更自然、更具表现力的对话式虚拟人提供了新范式，并展示了在聊天机器人 Avatar、语音驱动用户 Avatar 和动画导演界面等多种应用场景中的灵活性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从音频LLM token直接生成3D面部动画，属于虚拟人/说话头/面部重演方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Abstract: Recent advances in Audio-LLMs like GPT-4o have ushered in an era of conversational interaction with language models. Conversational avatars however, still seem robotic in facial expression and conversational flow, in part due to sequential stages of speech recognition, text generation, turn-based text response, speech synthesis, and audio driven facial animation. Based on our insight that audio-tokens produced by current Audio-LLMs carry sufficient information to reconstruct a plausible facial performance, we present TokTalk, a system that directly outputs expressive facial animation in real-time from streaming audio-tokens. We construct a novel audio-token to 3D facial motion dataset, on which TokTalk is trained using a Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching model. A lightweight adaptation strategy allows our trained model to seamlessly connect to any token-based Audio-LLM at minimal computational overhead. Our chunk-based processing further enables parametric trade-off between latency and facial quality, shown through ablation studies. We further show that the real-time performance of TokTalk is comparable in latency to prior art solutions, and significantly favorable (via a perceptual study) in terms of quality, expressivity and control of the 3D facial performance. We showcase TokTalk's flexibility using a chatbot Avatar, a voice-driven user Avatar, and an animation Director's interface, as diverse audio-visual face applications.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化肺结节诊断系统受限于多样化的标注数据稀缺。扩散生成模型虽可用于数据增强，但现有条件生成方法主要优化空间重建损失，仅关注体素级相似性，未能充分约束病灶级别的强度分布，导致生成结节纹理过于平滑，无法准确反映不同亚型结节的衰减特性（如实性、部分实性、磨玻璃结节的不同HU值分布）。

Method: 提出可控潜扩散模型，在潜空间中进行条件生成。模型以结节亚型、空间掩码和HU直方图作为条件输入，并引入可微的直方图正则化项（基于特征空间的直方图损失），在生成过程中约束体素强度分布与目标直方图一致。该方法在3D CT体积中合成结节，而非独立生成2D切片。

Result: 在肺部CT数据上的实验表明：该方法在定量指标和视觉图灵测试中均达到强视觉真实性；生成结节用于数据增强时，能提升下游临床任务性能，尤其对欠代表性结节亚型效果显著；对亚型感知的恶性分类任务也有潜在益处。

Conclusion: 直方图正则化的潜扩散模型能有效生成亚型一致、视觉逼真的肺结节，解决了现有方法强度分布建模不足的问题。该方法在数据增强和下游临床任务中展现出实用价值，为医学图像生成提供了新的可控性思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出可控潜扩散模型用于肺结节合成，属于扩散模型在医学图像生成中的应用。虽然具体任务是医学图像，但核心方法是条件潜扩散模型（LDM），与文生图/图像扩散这一[CANONICAL]子主题的扩散模型基础方法高度契合，且该子主题可涵盖各类条件图像生成任务。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical image synthesis for lung nodules, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes negative keywords like medical diagnosis. Signals: medical diagnosis, lung nodule synthesis, histogram regularization, diffusion model, data augmentation

Abstract: While automated diagnosis systems have achieved remarkable success in computed tomography (CT)-based lung cancer screening, their development remains limited by the scarcity of diverse, annotated pulmonary nodule datasets. Diffusion-based generative models offer a promising strategy for data synthesis; however, many existing conditional approaches primarily optimize spatial reconstruction losses, which encourage voxel-wise similarity but may inadequately constrain lesion-level intensity distributions. As a result, these methods may produce over-smoothed texture profiles and underrepresent the distinct attenuation characteristics of different nodule subtypes, including solid, part-solid, and ground-glass nodules. To address this challenge, we propose a controllable latent diffusion model that synthesizes pulmonary nodules within full 3D CT volumes while accurately modeling nodule-specific intensity distributions. Specifically, rather than relying solely on spatial losses, we introduce a histogram-based regularization term that constrains voxel intensity distributions during the generative process. The model combines subtype, spatial mask, and Hounsfield unit (HU) histogram conditioning with the differentiable feature-space histogram regularization term to better align lesion-level intensity distributions, improving the visual plausibility and subtype consistency of synthesized nodules. Extensive experiments on lung CT data demonstrate that our framework achieves strong visual realism, validated through both quantitative metrics and a visual Turing test. Furthermore, when used for data augmentation, the generated nodules improve performance in downstream clinical tasks, particularly for underrepresented nodule subtypes, and show a potential benefit for subtype-informed malignancy classification.

</details>

### [46] [OmniMem: Scalable and Adaptive Memory Retrieval for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30519)
*Lin Zhao, Yushu Wu, Yifan Gong, Yanzhi Wang, Pu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (87.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniMem 提出了一种显式全范围记忆检索框架，通过自适应窗口排除、查询共享KV选择和逐头分散KV访问，在自回归长视频生成中实现高效的稀疏KV检索，显著提升动态度并保持一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成在生成长视频时需要反复访问不断增长的KV缓存，现有方法通过截断或压缩为隐式记忆来减少开销，但都丢失了对查询相关历史细节的显式访问能力，导致长视频生成质量受限。

Method: OmniMem 包含三个关键设计：(1) 自适应窗口排除（Adaptive Window Exclusion）——在有足够长程历史时，从候选集中排除局部窗口块，将稀疏预算留给信息量更大的长程检索；(2) 查询共享KV选择（Query-Shared KV Selection）——减少跨查询的多样性，避免选择爆炸；(3) 逐头分散KV访问（Per-Head Scattered KV Access）——避免将头特定的选择扩展为大的KV缓冲区，允许每个注意力头根据自身选择模式检索非连续的KV块。

Result: 在长视频生成实验中，OmniMem 相比强基线将动态度（Dynamic Degree）提升了52.3%，同时保持了强一致性，且内存使用量相当。

Conclusion: OmniMem 通过显式全范围稀疏KV检索，有效解决了自回归长视频生成中历史信息访问的瓶颈，在保持内存效率的同时显著提升了生成视频的动态性和一致性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦长视频生成中的KV缓存检索优化，属于视频生成范畴。虽然论文使用自回归范式，但核心是视频生成中的内存检索机制，与文生视频/视频扩散方向（text_to_video_diffusion）最为接近，该方向涵盖视频生成方法。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient autoregressive video generation with a novel memory retrieval method, aligning with high-priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: autoregressive video generation, efficient inference, long video generation, sparse KV retrieval, dynamic degree improvement

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video generation extends videos by producing latent chunks sequentially, but scaling to long videos requires repeated access to a growing historical KV cache. Existing methods reduce this cost by truncating the KV cache or compressing it into implicit memory, but both lose explicit access to query-relevant historical details. We propose OmniMem, an explicit full-range memory retrieval framework that performs sparse KV retrieval over the historical cache. To make this practical for chunk-based AR video generation, OmniMem addresses two issues: (i) local bias in sparse KV selection and (ii) Union Explosion in memory access. Adaptive Window Exclusion removes local-window blocks from the selection candidates when sufficient long-range history is available, preserving the sparse budget for informative long-range retrieval. Query-Shared KV Selection reduces cross-query diversity, while Per-Head Scattered KV Access avoids expanding head-specific selections into a large selected KV buffer. This allows each attention head to retrieve non-contiguous KV blocks according to its own selection pattern. Experiments on long-video generation show that OmniMem improves Dynamic Degree by 52.3% and preserves strong consistency over strong baselines, while maintaining comparable memory usage.

</details>

*Haoyu Zhao, Jiaxi Gu, Haoran Chen, Qingping Zheng, Yeying Jin, Hongyi Yang, Junqi Cheng, Yuang Zhang, Zenghui Lu, Huan Yu, Jie Jiang, Peng Shu, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang*

TL;DR: CameraNoise 提出一种流到噪声的扭曲方法，将相机运动编码为时序一致的随机表示，通过几何引导重投影流和噪声扭曲算法在保持高斯先验的同时实现精确的相机控制，显著提升了视频扩散中相机轨迹的忠实度和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在精确相机位姿控制方面存在不足，直接注入数值相机参数的方法难以弥合抽象坐标与视觉内容之间的鸿沟，导致结构畸变。需要一种能将相机运动忠实编码到扩散过程中的方法。

Method: 提出 CameraNoise 框架，核心包括：(1) 几何引导重投影流 (Geometry-guided Reprojection Flow)，用于计算相机变换下的像素对应关系；(2) 噪声扭曲算法 (Noise Warping)，将相机运动编码到噪声空间中，在保持高斯先验的同时实现时序一致的噪声传播。该方法将相机位姿嵌入噪声空间而非直接注入扩散骨干网络，从而解耦运动与场景外观。

Result: 大量实验表明，CameraNoise 在视觉质量和轨迹忠实度上显著优于先前方法，能够生成稳定、高保真的视频，并精确遵循指定的相机运动轨迹。

Conclusion: CameraNoise 通过将相机控制嵌入噪声空间而非直接注入参数，有效解决了视频扩散中相机控制与几何一致性的难题，为视频生成中的精确相机操控提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CameraNoise，通过几何流引导的噪声扭曲实现视频扩散中的精确相机控制，属于视频扩散模型中的相机控制方法，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable video generation with a novel geometry-guided noise warping method, highly relevant to your priority on diffusion video generation and world models. Signals: video diffusion, camera control, noise warping, geometry-guided flow, trajectory faithfulness

Abstract: Precise camera pose control is critical for video diffusion, yet maintaining geometric consistency remains a challenge. Existing methods that directly inject numerical camera parameters into the diffusion backbone often fail to bridge the gap between abstract coordinates and visual content, leading to structural distortions. To address this issue, we propose CameraNoise, a flow-to-noise warping method that encodes camera motion into a temporally coherent stochastic representation. Unlike conventional conditioning, CameraNoise embeds camera poses directly into the noise space. This decouples motion from scene appearance while faithfully preserving trajectory dynamics. Specifically, we introduce a novel Geometry-guided Reprojection Flow and a noise warping algorithm, which jointly preserve the Gaussian prior of diffusion and ensure consistent noise propagation under camera transformations. By integrating CameraNoise into the diffusion process, our framework delivers stable, high-fidelity videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms prior methods in both visual quality and trajectory faithfulness. The project page and code are available at: .

</details>


### [48] [SlotMemory: Object-Centric KV Memory for Streaming Long-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31033)
*Weijia Dou, Hui Li, Jiahao Cui, Lei Zhou, Jingdong Wang, Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SlotMemory 提出了一种以物体为中心的键值记忆机制，将视频扩散 Transformer 的 KV 流分解为离散的语义槽，实现长视频流式生成中实体级持久化和提示感知检索，在 60 秒交互式叙事生成中显著提升了身份一致性和动态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频生成模型依赖时间中心记忆（原始帧、片段或未聚类 token），当物体离开画面或交互提示切换时，常出现身份漂移和语义不一致问题。作者认为需要从"何时发生"的记忆抽象转向"什么被表示"的物体级记忆。

Method: 将视频扩散 Transformer 的 KV 流分解为离散、可复用的语义槽（slot），每个槽作为路由地址索引和存储高保真 KV token，实现跨长时程的实体级持久化和提示感知检索。基于 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 骨干进行流式视频生成。

Result: 在 60 秒交互式叙事生成任务上，SlotMemory 达到 81.61 的质量分数，动态一致性相对最强基线提升 22.8%。

Conclusion: 结构化语义表示（而非原始时间容量）是实现持久长视频合成的关键原语。SlotMemory 通过物体级 KV 记忆有效缓解了身份漂移和语义不一致问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SlotMemory，一种面向流式长视频生成的物体级 KV 记忆机制，基于 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 视频扩散骨干进行长视频交互式生成。核心贡献在视频扩散模型的长视频流式生成，属于多模态生成方向下的文生视频与视频扩散（text_to_video_diffusion）子主题。

Importance reason: Proposes object-centric KV memory for streaming video generation, directly addressing identity consistency in long videos, a key challenge in your priority subtopics. Signals: video generation, autoregressive video generation, world model, efficient inference, controllable generation

Abstract: Streaming video generation models typically rely on temporal-centric memory, which organizes historical context as raw frames, chunk segments, or unclustered tokens. This organization frequently leads to identity drift and semantic inconsistency when entities exit the frame or during interactive prompt transitions. To address these limitations, we propose SlotMemory, an object-centric Key-Value memory mechanism for streaming video diffusion. Our approach shifts the memory abstraction from "when" an event occurred to "what" is being represented by decomposing the transformer's key-value manifold into discrete, reusable semantic slots. By utilizing these slots as routing addresses to index and store high-fidelity key-value tokens, we enable entity-level persistence and prompt-aware retrieval across long horizons. Evaluated on 60-second interactive narratives using the Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B backbone, SlotMemory achieves a state-of-the-art quality score of 81.61 and a 22.8 percent relative improvement in dynamic consistency over the strongest existing streaming baseline. Our results demonstrate that structured semantic representation, rather than raw temporal capacity, is the essential primitive for persistent long-form video synthesis. Our codes and checkpoints are available at .

Method: 提出 FREUD 框架：1) 逐帧编码器（Frame-wise Encoder）对每一帧雷达回波独立编码，支持连续更新；2) 统一视频解码器（United Decoder）处理所有帧的潜变量以保持时序一致性；3) 不确定性保持的第一阶段通过集成（ensembling）捕获偶然不确定性；4) 在紧凑潜空间中使用 rectified flow transformer 进行概率生成。

Result: 在 SEVIR 基准上达到降水临近预报的 SOTA 性能，并通过模型规模扩展和测试时计算扩展进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: FREUD 证明了基于 rectified flow 的紧凑潜空间生成模型在概率性天气临近预报中的有效性，不确定性保持的编码器设计对极端天气事件预测尤为重要。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 rectified flow transformer 进行降水临近预报（precipitation nowcasting），本质上是条件视频生成任务（以雷达回波序列为条件生成未来帧）。这属于视频扩散/流匹配生成方法，与 canonical subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散" 高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Strong probabilistic nowcasting with rectified flow transformers, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive video generation. Signals: rectified flow transformer, probabilistic nowcasting, uncertainty-preserving first stage, SEVIR benchmark SOTA, frame-wise encoding for continuous updates

Abstract: Accurate weather forecasts are essential across various domains and are safety-critical in extreme weather conditions. Compared to simulation-based forecasting, data-driven approaches show greater efficiency, enabling short-term, high-resolution nowcasting. In particular, diffusion models proved effective in weather nowcasting due to their strong probabilistic foundation. However, existing methods rely on deterministic compression to reduce the complexity of high-dimensional weather data, limiting their ability to capture uncertainty in the decoding process. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{FREUD}$, a $\textbf{Fr}$ame-wise $\textbf{E}$ncoder and $\textbf{U}$nited $\textbf{D}$ecoder model based on rectified flow transformers for efficient compression of spatio-temporal weather data. Frame-wise encoding enables continuous forecast updates, while the unified video decoder ensures temporal consistency. Our uncertainty-preserving first stage allows us to capture aleatoric uncertainty via ensembling, which is particularly beneficial for extreme weather events with high decoding variability. We achieve state-of-the-art performance in precipitation nowcasting with a compact latent-space rectified flow transformer on the SEVIR benchmark and show further performance gains by model and test-time scaling. Code available here:

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型在生成包含多个事件的长时间视频时表现不佳，难以在单个视频中连贯地呈现多个不同事件。作者希望在不重新训练模型的前提下，利用扩散过程的内在特性来引导多事件视频生成。

Method: 首先通过分析 DiT 去噪轨迹，发现存在"转折点"——在该点之前文本条件影响全局布局，之后影响细粒度细节。基于此提出两个引导手柄：(1) 事件分区掩码 (Event-Partitioned Masking)：在去噪早期强制事件边界，同时允许跨事件过渡带；(2) 跨事件提示融合 (Cross-Event Prompt Fusion)：在后期阶段注入相邻事件语义进行细化。同时自建了多事件生成评测套件 Meve。

Result: 在 8 个指标上达到 SOTA，优于其他无需训练的方法。文本对齐度随事件数量增加而提升，表明该方法具有随事件数扩展的潜力。在视频一致性和事件分离之间提供了可调节的权衡。

Conclusion: TunerDiT 证明了通过理解扩散过程的内在特性，可以在不训练的情况下有效引导视频 DiT 生成多事件视频，为长视频可控生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TunerDiT，一种无需训练的多事件视频生成渐进式引导方法，基于视频扩散 Transformer (DiT)，属于文本到视频生成方向。映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "文生图与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion），因为论文核心是视频 DiT 的推理时引导方法，属于视频扩散生成范畴。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable multi-event video generation with a training-free DiT steering method, highly relevant to your priority on video generation and diffusion transformers. Signals: training-free progressive steering, diffusion transformer (DiT), multi-event video generation, event-partitioned masking, cross-event prompt fusion

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation faces challenging questions when generating videos with long horizons containing multiple events. Inspired by the intrinsics of the diffusion process, we probe video diffusion transformers (DiTs) and uncover intrinsic turning points in the DiT denoising trajectory where conditioning text affects generation from global layout to fine-grained details. Building on this finding, we present TunerDiT, a simple yet effective progressive steering method that requires no additional training for multi-event generation. TunerDiT comprises two steering handles: (1) Event-Partitioned Masking that enforces event boundaries while allowing cross-event transition bands; (2) Cross-Event Prompt Fusion that injects neighboring event semantics for late-stage refinement. We contribute a self-curated prompt suite for benchmarking multi-event generation, i.e., Meve. TunerDiT achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 metrics and offers a tunable trade-off between video consistency and event separation, compared with other training-free methods. The improvement in text alignment increases with the event count, indicating a scaling possibility with increasing event count.

</details>

TL;DR: LVSA是一种无需训练、模型无关的块稀疏注意力方法，通过结构化窗口模式结合旋转全局锚点，显著降低长视频扩散Transformer的计算开销（最高3.33x加速），同时消除长距离时序伪影，并能在单GPU上生成长于训练时长的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频扩散推理中，密集自注意力的计算成本随序列长度二次增长，且超出训练时长后模型输出趋于静态重复（"冻结"现象）。现有方法要么需要重新训练成本过高，要么无法同时满足性能和质量的规模化要求。

Method: 提出Long Video Sparse Attention (LVSA)，一种无需训练的块稀疏注意力机制。它结合结构化窗口模式与旋转全局锚点，打破固定网格偏置，消除长距离时序伪影。配合FlashInfer内核实现高效计算，并适配NPU平台。

Result: 在Wan 2.1 1.3B上实现6x时长下3.17x加速，Wan 2.1 14B上2.98x加速，HunyuanVideo 1.5上1.5x时长下3.33x加速。相比RIFLEx和UltraViCo分别提升2.41x和3.27x。在NPU上最高加速2.71x。同时引入VQeval工具公平评估循环视频失败。

Conclusion: LVSA在训练时长内质量中性，在扩展时长下质量正向，有效解决了长视频扩散推理的计算瓶颈和时序伪影问题，是一种可扩展的高效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出LVSA，一种无需训练的块稀疏注意力机制，用于加速长视频扩散模型推理，属于生成模型效率优化，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Training-free sparse attention for long video diffusion directly addresses efficient inference and video generation, both highly weighted in your priorities. Signals: training-free, block-sparse attention, long video diffusion, up to 3.33x speedup, single GPU extended generation

Abstract: Dense self-attention is the compute and quality bottleneck of long-video diffusion inference: cost grows quadratically with the sequence length, and beyond the training horizon the model converges to near-static output, that is, "frozen" repetitive video. State of the art approaches are either too costly, e.g., they require retraining, or fail to satisfy both performance and quality objectives in a scalable manner. To this end, we introduce Long Video Sparse Attention (LVSA), a training-free model-agnostic block-sparse attention for video diffusion transformers that combines a structured window pattern with rotating global anchors, thus removing the fixed-grid bias which causes long-range temporal artifacts. LVSA, combined with a FlashInfer kernel, reduces compute up to 3.17x on Wan 2.1 1.3B at a 6x horizon, 2.98x on Wan 2.1 14B at a 6x horizon, and 3.33x on HunyuanVideo 1.5 at a 1.5x horizon, compared to dense attention. Beyond reducing compute, LVSA enables HunyuanVideo 1.5 generation at a 2x horizon, which is otherwise out-of-memory on a single GPU. Moreover, LVSA provides speedups up to 2.41x compared to RIFLEx and 3.27x compared to UltraViCo on Wan 2.1 1.3B. To demonstrate applicability across diverse platforms, we apply LVSA on NPUs and achieve speedups up to 2.71x on Wan 2.2 A14B and 3.24x on Wan 2.1 1.3B compared to dense attention. To evaluate quality in a fair way, we introduce VQeval, a tool properly scoring loopy video failures, which instead are rewarded in state of the art evaluators like VBench-Long. LVSA is quality-neutral for generation at training horizon length and quality-positive at extended lengths.

</details>


### [52] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele, Yiming Qin, Alba Carballo-Castro, Justin Deschenaux, Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs)，用固定点求解器替代部分去噪器实现自适应深度，配合跨步一致性损失和三态重用（3SR）策略，在减少参数、训练时间和显存的同时，显著提升低预算掩码生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码生成模型（MGM）虽支持并行解码且跨模态表现优异，但每一步都需要全序列双向 Transformer，训练成本高且低采样预算下质量下降严重。现有改进方法仍为每个精炼步骤分配固定计算量，缺乏自适应深度机制。

Method: 提出 CoFRe 框架：(1) 用固定点求解器替代部分去噪器层，通过共享注意力层实现自适应深度；(2) 跨步一致性损失（cross-step consistency loss）对齐相邻去噪步的隐藏表示；(3) 三态重用（3SR）策略，对未改变、仍掩码和新揭示的 token 分别处理，用前一步解热启动求解器。支持预训练 MGM 通过短微调转换为 FP-MGM。

Result: 在 OpenWebText 上，参数减少 38.8%，训练时间减少 11.5%，显存减少 16.9%，在 96 次 Transformer 块前向传播预算下生成困惑度从 830.8 降至 101.8。在 ImageNette 上，训练时间减少 48.6%，显存减少 50.7%，所有采样预算下 FID 均有提升。

Conclusion: CoFRe 提供了一种实用的框架，能够以更低的训练成本和更强的低预算生成质量实现掩码生成，且支持从预训练模型高效转换。

Classification reason: 该论文提出固定点掩码生成模型（FP-MGM），通过固定点求解器替代部分去噪器实现自适应深度，减少参数和训练成本，提升低预算采样质量。这属于生成模型效率优化（加速、少步采样、蒸馏等），因此映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel fixed-point solver for adaptive-depth masked generation, directly improving efficiency and low-budget quality, aligning with model efficiency and generation priorities. Signals: fixed-point solver, adaptive depth, cross-step consistency loss, three-state reuse, parameter reduction

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>

*Paul Caucheteux, Clément Bonet, Anna Korba*

TL;DR: 本文提出了生成式Wasserstein流（GWF）的统一理论框架，证明多种现有生成模型（扩散模型、GAN等）可以视为f-散度目标下参数化JKO格式的实例，并将框架扩展到积分概率度量（IPM）和最大均值差异（MMD），推导出新的基于JKO的生成算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽然都可以看作最小化分布间散度，但依赖不同的算法和几何原理，缺乏统一的视角。作者希望建立一个统一的Wasserstein梯度流理论框架，揭示不同生成方法之间的内在联系，并推导新的生成算法。

Method: 基于Wasserstein梯度流的JKO（Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto）格式，将生成建模视为参数化分布空间中的变分优化问题。作者证明扩散模型、得分匹配、GAN等可统一为f-散度下参数化JKO的实例，并将框架扩展到IPM和MMD，推导新的JKO生成算法。

Result: 建立了多种生成模型的统一理论框架，揭示了它们之间的等价关系；通过实验验证了JKO正则化对不同目标函数的影响；分析了参数化Wasserstein流中动力学受限于参数化映射诱导分布时的行为。

Conclusion: GWF框架为理解和设计生成模型提供了统一的理论基础，有助于未来开发更高效的生成算法，并揭示了不同生成范式之间的深层联系。

Classification reason: 该论文提出生成式Wasserstein流（GWF）的统一理论框架，将多种生成模型（扩散模型、GAN等）统一为JKO格式的变分推断，核心关注生成模型的训练与采样效率优化，与"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Unifies diverse generative models under Wasserstein flow theory, offering potential efficiency insights for continuous multimodal models. Signals: theoretical unification, generative model theory, JKO scheme, f-divergence, IPM

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>

### [54] [Mental Damage: Caption Poisoning Attacks on Retrieval-Augmented Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30365)
*Yizhu Wen, Shuhao Zhang, Nan Zhang, Long Cheng, Hanqing Guo*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对检索增强文生音乐（TTM）系统的字幕投毒攻击方法。攻击者通过向音乐知识库注入少量精心构造的音乐字幕，使系统检索到恶意字幕，从而偏置提示增强过程，使生成结果偏离用户意图。该方法采用双层字幕投毒策略，在保留高层检索锚点的同时注入低层声学描述符，实现对生成结果的定向操控。实验表明，该方法能有效使生成结果向攻击者目标靠近，同时保持与原始用户查询的相当对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强的文生音乐系统依赖于音乐知识库中字幕数据的完整性。现有研究未充分关注这种依赖关系带来的安全风险——攻击者可以通过污染知识库中的字幕数据来操控生成结果。本文旨在揭示这一安全漏洞，并展示其实际可行性。

Method: 提出双层字幕投毒策略（dual-layer caption poisoning strategy）：第一层保留高层检索锚点（如音乐风格、流派等宏观特征），确保恶意字幕能被检索到；第二层注入低层声学描述符（如节奏、音色、乐器等微观特征），以偏置提示增强过程，使下游MusicGen生成器朝向攻击者指定的目标意图。实验使用MusicCaps知识库、CLAP检索器和MusicGen生成管线。

Result: 在MusicCaps知识库、CLAP检索器和MusicGen管线上的实验表明，被投毒后的生成结果显著向攻击者目标靠近，同时与原始用户查询的对齐度保持相当水平。这证明了检索增强文生音乐系统存在实际的安全完整性风险。

Conclusion: 检索增强的创意AI系统（如文生音乐）存在因知识库字幕投毒而导致的安全风险。攻击者无需修改用户提示、检索器或生成器，仅通过注入少量恶意字幕即可有效操控生成结果。该工作揭示了这类系统在数据完整性方面的脆弱性，为未来构建更安全的检索增强生成系统提供了警示。

Classification reason: 该论文研究检索增强文生音乐系统的投毒攻击，属于生成模型的安全与可控性问题。最佳匹配方向为多模态生成（multimodal_generation），同时涉及多模态理解与生成（检索增强中的caption理解）。现有CANONICAL subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）涵盖生成模型的对齐、可控性与安全性，该论文的caption投毒攻击正是破坏生成对齐与可控性的攻击方法，因此复用该subtopic。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented text-to-music (TTM) systems augment underspecified user prompts using captions retrieved from a music caption dataset. This design introduces an integrity dependency on the music knowledge database. We show that an attacker can poison the database by injecting a small number of crafted music captions, causing the system to retrieve malicious captions that bias prompt augmentation and steer generation away from the user's intended function, without modifying the user prompt, retriever, or generator. To achieve the music caption poisoning attack, we propose a dual-layer caption poisoning strategy that preserves high-level retrieval anchors while injecting low-level acoustic descriptors to steer prompt augmentation and downstream music generation toward an attacker-chosen target intent. In a MusicCaps knowledge database, CLAP retriever, and MusicGen pipeline, poisoned generations move substantially closer to the attacker's target, while remaining comparably aligned with the original user query. These results expose a practical integrity risk for retrieval-augmented creative AI systems. Our demo can be found at:

Result: LGC 在 5000 次查询下达到 SSIM>0.99、LPIPS<0.01 的视觉保真度，同时保持高攻击成功率，成功攻破对抗训练过的鲁棒模型，并展现出跨数据集迁移能力，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: While decision-based black-box adversarial attacks present a severe security threat, current methodologies suffer from fundamental limitations. Pixel-wise attacks frequently introduce unnatural, high-frequency visual artifacts, while latent-space frameworks are confined by the limited search space of low-dimensional manifolds and inherent reconstruction flaws. To resolve these limitations, we propose Latent Geometric Chords (LGC) for Query-Efficient Decision-Based Adversarial Attacks alongside a variant, LGC-H. At its core, LGC navigates decision boundaries by executing a curvature-aware geometric search within a compressed semantic manifold. To guarantee high visual fidelity and circumvent dimensionality bottlenecks, we introduce a Residual-based Adversarial Generation (RAG) mechanism. RAG isolates semantic perturbations as geometric chords and superimposes them directly onto the original source image. RAG substantially resolves baseline reconstruction flaws and effectively doubles the permissible search space dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that LGC achieves robust cross-dataset transferability and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, our method, LGC, minimizes perturbation magnitudes while achieving state-of-the-art visual fidelity--with a Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) exceeding 0.99 and a Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) below 0.01 at 5000 queries--and sustaining high attack success rates under stringent perceptual constraints, successfully compromising adversarially trained robust models. The source code is available at: .

</details>

TL;DR: 提出 PATHS 方法，利用并行回火（Parallel Tempering）对扩散/流生成模型进行推理时奖励对齐的初始采样，通过多链耦合和 Metropolis 交换克服标准 SMC 方法在高复杂奖励景观中陷入局部模式的问题，在布局到图像和数量感知生成任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时奖励对齐（inference-time reward alignment）可以在不重新训练的情况下引导预训练扩散/流模型满足用户指定的奖励。SMC（序贯蒙特卡洛）方法在此任务中表现突出，但其从标准先验初始化粒子，而高奖励区域在复杂奖励景观中极为稀少；即使最近的奖励感知初始化方法也容易陷入多模态奖励景观的局部模式。因此需要一种更有效的初始化策略来探索稀有高奖励区域。

Method: 提出 PATHS（Parallel Tempering for High-complexity reward Sampling），构建一个奖励温度阶梯（reward-tempered ladder），维护多条不同温度下的采样链，温度越高奖励景观越平坦。链之间周期性地执行 Metropolis 交换，使低温链（目标分布）能够借助高温链的探索能力跳出局部模式。该方法作为 SMC 推理时对齐的初始化步骤，提升粒子对高奖励区域的覆盖。

Result: 在布局到图像生成（layout-to-image）和数量感知生成（quantity-aware generation）任务上，PATHS 在多个奖励指标上一致优于标准 SMC 和现有奖励感知初始化方法，尤其在复杂提示（复杂奖励景观）上提升显著。

Conclusion: 并行回火是一种有效的推理时奖励对齐初始化策略，能够显著增强有限预算下对稀有高奖励区域的探索，缓解多模态奖励景观中的模式捕获问题，为扩散/流模型的奖励对齐提供了新的改进方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理时奖励对齐（inference-time reward alignment），通过并行回火（parallel tempering）改进扩散/流模型的初始采样，以更好地满足用户指定的奖励函数。这属于生成模型对齐与奖励优化的范畴，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" 高度匹配。

Abstract: Inference-time reward alignment steers pretrained diffusion and flow-based generative models to satisfy user-specified rewards without retraining. Recently, Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) has emerged as a powerful framework for this task by iteratively filtering and propagating multiple particles. However, we show that standard SMC-based methods often suffer from poor performance because they initialize particles from a standard prior, whereas high-reward regions in complex reward landscapes are extremely rare. Further, we show that even recent reward-aware initial sampling approaches remain vulnerable to getting trapped in local modes, as complex reward landscapes are often multi-modal. To overcome these limitations, we propose PATHS (PArallel Tempering for High-complexity reward Sampling), a novel initialization method that couples multiple sampling chains through parallel tempering. PATHS maintains a ladder of reward-tempered chains and periodically performs Metropolis swaps, enabling efficient exploration across flattened reward landscapes, thereby mitigating the mode-trapping issues. Our analysis reveals that this mechanism substantially enhances the finite-budget exploration of rare, high-reward regions that are typically challenging to sample. Experiments on layout-to-image and quantity-aware generation show that PATHS achieves consistent gains in alignment quality, particularly on complex prompts.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表是传达复杂研究思想最有效的方式之一，但制作出版级质量的插图仍然是论文准备中最耗时的环节。现有自动化系统仅针对单一图表类型且仅支持文本输入，无法覆盖研究人员实际使用的多样图表类型和输入条件，且光栅输出无法局部修改。

Method: 提出 Crafter 多智能体框架，通过编排多个 LLM/VLM 智能体协作完成科学图表生成，无需架构修改即可泛化到不同图表类型和输入条件。CraftEditor 应用相同模式将光栅输出转换为可编辑 SVG。CraftBench 基准涵盖三种图表类型（模型架构图、数据流图、结果图）和四种输入条件（仅文本、仅图像、文本+图像、参考图），并有人工质量标注。

Result: 在 PaperBanana-Bench 和 CraftBench 上，Crafter 显著优于独立生成器和智能体基线。消融实验证实了每个组件的独立贡献。CraftEditor 能忠实将输出转换为可编辑 SVG，超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 科学图表是结构化语义组件的组合，局部错误需要的是编排框架而非更强的骨干网络。Crafter 多智能体框架有效解决了科学图表生成的多样性和可编辑性问题，为自动化科学图表生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scientific figures are among the most effective means of communicating complex research ideas, yet producing publication-quality illustrations remains one of the most labor-intensive parts of paper preparation. Existing automated systems each target a single figure type under text-only input, leaving the diversity of types and conditions researchers actually use unaddressed; their raster outputs further cannot be locally revised. Because scientific figures are structured compositions of discrete semantic components, the localized errors generators produce on such layouts demand not a stronger backbone but a harness. We instantiate this harness in two complementary systems: Crafter, a multi-agent harness for figure generation that generalizes across figure types and input conditions without architectural changes, and CraftEditor, which applies the same pattern to convert raster outputs into editable SVGs. Moreover, we introduce CraftBench, a benchmark spanning three figure types and four input conditions with human quality annotation. Experiments show that Crafter substantially outperforms both standalone generators and the agentic baseline on PaperBanana-Bench and CraftBench, with ablations confirming each component's independent contribution; CraftEditor faithfully converts outputs into editable SVGs that surpass all baselines. Our code and benchmark are available at .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dual-Spectral Flow Matching (DSFM) 框架，通过小波变换和离散余弦变换将 fMRI BOLD 信号转换为双频谱表示，再使用谱流匹配模型生成类条件余弦频率表示，最后通过逆变换重建生理上合理的 fMRI 时间序列，用于脑疾病识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI 数据采集成本高、样本有限，现有生成模型难以复现 BOLD 信号的非平稳性、复杂时空动态和生理变化，亟需一种能生成高保真 fMRI 时间序列的方法。

Method: 提出 DSFM 框架：1) 通过离散小波变换 (DWT) 将 BOLD 信号转换为小波分解图，捕获全局瞬态和多尺度变化；2) 通过离散余弦变换 (DCT) 在脑区和时间维度上投影，利用低频主导 BOLD 系数的局部能量压缩；3) 训练谱流匹配 (spectral flow matching) 模型生成类条件余弦频率表示；4) 通过逆 DCT 和逆 DWT 重建时域 BOLD 信号。

Result: 在 fMRI 脑网络分类下游任务中，DSFM 生成的样本提升了分类性能，验证了生成信号在保留生理动态方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 双变换方法通过施加结构化频率先验，能够生成生理上合理的 fMRI 时间序列，为数据驱动的脑分析模型提供了高质量合成数据。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging (fMRI) provides non-invasive access to dynamic brain activity by measuring blood oxygen level-dependent (BOLD) signals over time. However, the resource-intensive nature of fMRI acquisition limits the availability of high-fidelity samples required for data-driven brain analysis models. While modern generative models can synthesize fMRI data, they often remain challenging in replicating their inherent non-stationarity, intricate spatiotemporal dynamics, and physiological variations of raw BOLD signals. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Spectral Flow Matching (DSFM), a novel fMRI generative framework that cascades dual frequency representation of BOLD signals with spectral flow matching. Specifically, our framework first converts BOLD signals into a wavelet decomposition map via a discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to capture globalized transient and multi-scale variations, and projects into the discrete cosine transform (DCT) space across brain regions and time to exploit localized energy compaction of low-frequency dominant BOLD coefficients. Subsequently, a spectral flow matching model is trained to generate class-conditioned cosine-frequency representation. The generated samples are reconstructed through inverse DCT and inverse DWT operations to recover physiologically plausible time-domain BOLD signals. This dual-transform approach imposes structured frequency priors and preserves key physiological brain dynamics. Ultimately, we demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through improved downstream fMRI-based brain network classification. The code is available at .

</details>

Importance reason: Focuses on image restoration dataset generation, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Multimodal foundation models for data generation, Real-world image restoration, GGT-100K dataset, Nano-Banana-2, VLM-based adaptive prompting

Abstract: Real-world image restoration (IR) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality paired training data. Synthetic datasets are abundant but often fail to model real-world degradations, while real-world paired datasets are expensive and difficult to capture. As a result, IR models trained on these datasets show limited generalization in real-world scenarios. In this work, we propose Generative Ground Truth (GGT) by using generative multimodal foundation models (MFMs) to produce high-quality (HQ) targets from real-world low-quality (LQ) images. We first conduct a systematic evaluation of nine state-of-the-art MFMs, including Nano-Banana-2 and GPT-Image-2, on images of various scenes and degradation types. The results demonstrate that Nano-Banana-2 with VLM-based adaptive prompting shows the highest capability to synthesize perceptually realistic and content-faithful HQ targets, which can serve as the GGT for the LQ input. We then employ Nano-Banana-2 to build a GGT synthesis pipeline, which involves multi-stage quality control to ensure data reliability, and construct GGT-100K, an LQ-HQ paired dataset comprising 103,707 training pairs and covering diverse scenes and complex real-world degradations. A test set of 500 image pairs is also established. Extensive experiments show that GGT-100K consistently improves the real-world generalization of a wide range of IR models, with particularly strong benefits for finetuning generative models for IR tasks. Our results suggest that MFMs can serve as practical tools for restoration-oriented data generation, and GGT-100K is a useful resource to expand the generalization boundaries of real-world IR models.

</details>

Conclusion: 当前文生图模型在算术教育场景中缺乏数值和关系结构的精确建模能力，未来模型需要更强的数值和关系基础能力。E2V-Bench为这一方向提供了有效的评测工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了方程到视觉生成任务（equation-to-visual generation）并构建了E2V-Bench基准，用于评测文生图模型在算术教育场景中的视觉正确性。这属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配。同时该任务也涉及多模态理解（视觉问答/教育内容理解），因此也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on educational text-to-image generation, which is not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: Educational application, T2I benchmark, Object counting errors, Relational structure, Not core direction

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to support educational content creation, yet it remains unclear whether they can generate outputs that faithfully represent the pedagogical concepts they are intended to teach. Thus, we introduce equation-to-visual generation, a task that, in contrast to conventional image generation, requires producing pedagogically meaningful visuals from arithmetic equations while precisely preserving their numerical and relational structure. Informed by interviews with teachers and an analysis of educational materials, we construct E2V-Bench, a benchmark spanning four pedagogically grounded visual types, along with automatic metrics for evaluating visual correctness. Our evaluation reveals that recent text-to-image (T2I) models frequently fail on this task, with errors dominated by incorrect object counts and broken relational structure. Building on this, we explore benchmark-guided enhancement strategies. These strategies improve representative models, while the remaining gap calls for stronger numerical and relational grounding in future T2I models.

</details>


### [61] [Conformal Reliability: A New Evaluation Metric for Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30807)
*Yachen Gao, Xinwei Sun, Yikai Wang, Ye Shi, Jingya Wang, Jianfeng Feng, Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (70.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共形预测的可靠性评分（reliability score）作为条件生成模型的新评测指标，并设计了 CReL 框架来高效计算该指标。该指标衡量预测集在指定置信水平下的最差性能，弥补了现有单输出评测指标无法捕捉生成不确定性和风险的不足。在合成数据和图像到文本、文本到图像任务上的实验验证了该指标的有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成模型的评测指标通常只评估单个输出，无法捕捉生成过程中的不确定性和潜在风险。需要一种能够考虑模型固有不确定性、衡量生成可靠性的新评测指标。

Method: 提出基于共形预测（conformal prediction）的可靠性评分，定义预测集在指定置信水平下的最差性能。为解决高维输出空间和非凸优化难题，设计了 CReL（Conformal ReLiability）框架，包含两个步骤：(i) 构建具有期望覆盖率的预测集；(ii) 在预测集内优化可靠性评分。提供了覆盖率的理论保证。

Result: 在合成数据和图像到文本、文本到图像任务上的实验表明，CReL 能构建比现有方法更具信息量的预测集，可靠性评分作为评测指标具有良好的可解释性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的可靠性评分和 CReL 框架为条件生成模型提供了一种新的、考虑不确定性的评测方式，弥补了现有评测指标的不足，具有理论保证和实际有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于共形预测的可靠性评分作为条件生成模型的新评测指标，属于生成模型评测范畴，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a new evaluation metric for conditional generation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: new evaluation metric, conformal prediction, conditional generation, reliability score, image-to-text

Abstract: Conditional generative models have recently achieved remarkable success in various applications. However, a suitable metric for evaluating the reliability of these models, which takes into account their inherent uncertainty, is still lacking. Existing metrics, which typically assess a single output, may fail to capture the variability or potential risks in generation. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation metric called reliability score based on conformal prediction, which measures the worst-case performance within the prediction set at a pre-specified confidence level. However, computing this score is challenging due to the high-dimensional nature of the output space and the nonconvexity of both the metric function and the prediction set. To efficiently compute this score, we introduce Conformal ReLiability (CReL), a framework that can (i) construct the prediction set with desired coverage; and (ii) accurately optimize the reliability score within the constructed prediction set. We provide theoretical results on coverage and demonstrate empirically that our method produces more informative prediction sets than existing approaches. Experiments on synthetic data and the image-to-text and text-to-image tasks further demonstrate the interpretability of our new metric, and the validity and effectiveness of our computational framework. Source code can be found at .

</details>

### [62] [Equivariant Latent Alignment via Flow Matching under Group Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30705)
*Sunghyun Kim, Jaehoon Hahm, Jeongwoo Shin, Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流

Importance: 高优先级 (89.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Residual Latent Flow，一种基于 flow matching 的框架，用于校正潜空间中等变表示学习中的潜对齐偏差（latent misalignment），从而提升新视角合成质量。实验表明该方法在 SO(n) 旋转群下显著减少了潜空间不对齐问题，并改善了生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等变表示学习方法在构建潜空间时，理论上要求群变换在潜空间中线性作用，但实际学习到的潜变量往往存在"潜不对齐"（latent misalignment）问题，即预期的群作用与实际所需的变换之间存在偏差，导致等变关系无法被一致保持，影响新视角合成等下游任务的质量。

Method: 提出 Residual Latent Flow 框架：首先通过等变编码器将输入映射到潜空间，然后利用 flow matching 学习一个残差流（residual flow）来校正潜变量，使其更好地满足等变约束。该流模型在潜空间中沿群轨道（group orbit）进行校正，使得校正后的潜变量对群变换具有更一致的等变响应。最终解码器从校正后的潜变量重建输出。

Result: 在 SO(2) 和 SO(3) 旋转群下的新视角合成任务上，该方法显著降低了潜不对齐度量（latent misalignment metric），并提升了生成图像的 PSNR、SSIM 等指标，优于现有的等变生成模型和标准新视角合成基线。

Conclusion: Residual Latent Flow 通过 flow matching 有效校正了等变潜空间中的不对齐问题，为几何感知生成模型提供了一种通用且有效的潜空间校正机制，在提升等变一致性和生成质量方面具有广泛潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于 flow matching 的残差潜流（Residual Latent Flow）来校正潜空间中的等变对齐问题，用于新视角合成。核心涉及连续潜空间中的 flow matching 与视觉 latent 对齐，与 canonical 子主题 "连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流"（continuous_vlm_latent_flow）高度匹配。同时论文也属于多模态生成方向（新视角合成）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses continuous latent representation alignment via flow matching, a core subtopic, but focuses on equivariance for novel view synthesis rather than the user's priority of multimodal generation. Signals: flow matching, continuous latent representation, equivariant representation learning, latent misalignment correction, SO(n) rotation groups

Abstract: Geometry-aware generative models and novel view synthesis approaches have shown strong potential in visual fidelity and consistency. In parallel, equivariant representation learning has emerged as a powerful framework for constructing latent spaces where analytically known group transformations could act directly, capturing geometric structure in data and enhancing both interpretability and generalization in novel view synthesis. However, we identify that existing approaches often suffer from latent misalignment, a discrepancy between the intended group action and the actually required transformations in the latent space. Consequently, the learned latents often fail to consistently preserve the equivariant relations imposed by the underlying group symmetry. To address this, we propose Residual Latent Flow, a flow-based framework that corrects the misaligned latents, thereby improving compliance with the underlying equivariance relation. Our comprehensive experiments show that our method significantly reduces latent misalignment and improves novel view synthesis quality, under rotation groups SO(n).

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间音频合成技术在生成质量与推理延迟之间存在权衡，且难以从多模态输入中准确捕捉空间信息，缺乏实时流式生成能力。

Result: 在视频到空间音频和文本到空间音频两个任务上均取得了优于现有方法的性能，实现了实时流式生成。

Conclusion: SwanSphere 有效解决了空间音频生成中质量与延迟的权衡问题，通过多模态对齐和偏好优化实现了高保真流式空间音频合成，为沉浸式体验提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从全景视频和文本提示生成高质量空间音频，属于跨模态条件音频生成，与 [CANONICAL] 音频与多模态联合生成 (audio_av_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel autoregressive diffusion transformer for streaming spatial audio generation, but only tangentially touches priority subtopics like autoregressive generation and multimodal models. Signals: autoregressive diffusion transformer, streaming spatial audio, multimodal generation, spatial video-audio contrastive learning, online direct preference optimization

Abstract: Real-time and accurate spatial audio generation is pivotal for delivering an immersive experience. However, existing spatial audio synthesis technologies are often encumbered by a tradeoff between generation quality and high inference latency, as well as difficulty in capturing precise spatial information from multimodal inputs. To address these challenges, we propose SwanSphere, a unified streaming framework for high-fidelity spatial audio generation from panoramic videos and text prompts. SwanSphere mainly makes the following contributions: 1) We introduce a causal autoregressive diffusion transformer architecture that enables streaming high-quality spatial audio generation. 2) We design a Spatial Video-Audio Contrastive (SVAC) learning strategy to align the video encoder with the acoustic domain, and further employ a multi-objective online direct preference optimization (ODPO) scheme, resulting in strong spatial perception and robust multimodal spatial audio synthesis. 3) To alleviate the current scarcity of spatial audio datasets, we also develop an automated annotation pipeline for generating detailed spatial captions. Experimental results demonstrate that SwanSphere achieves superior performance in both video-to-spatial and text-to-spatial audio generation tasks. Demos can be found at: .

</details>


### [64] [ImmersiveTTS: Environment-Aware Text-to-Speech with Multimodal Diffusion Transformer and Domain-Specific Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30965)
*Jun-Hak Yun, Seung-Bin Kim, Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

TL;DR: ImmersiveTTS 是一种环境感知的文本到语音模型，基于多模态扩散Transformer和领域特定表征对齐，能够生成与环境上下文自然融合的语音，在自然度、可懂度和音频保真度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导音频生成在音效、语音、音乐等领域取得了进展，但联合生成语音与环境音频仍具挑战，因为两者在声学模式和时序动态上存在固有差异。现有方法难以实现语音与环境音频的自然融合。

Method: 基于多模态扩散Transformer，通过联合注意力机制融合文本对齐的语音潜变量与文本条件的环境上下文；引入领域特定表征对齐目标，利用语音和音频编码器的互补自监督表征增强语义一致性。

Result: 在客观指标和人类听力测试中，ImmersiveTTS 在自然度、可懂度和音频保真度方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模跨模态交互和领域特定表征对齐，可以有效实现环境感知的语音生成，为TTS与环境音频的联合生成提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出环境感知的文本到语音（TTS）模型，利用多模态扩散Transformer联合生成语音与环境音频，属于音频生成与多模态联合生成范畴，与CANONICAL subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel multimodal diffusion transformer for environment-aware TTS, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords not triggered. Signals: multimodal diffusion transformer, domain-specific representation alignment, environment-aware TTS, cross-modal joint attention, speech-audio fusion

Abstract: Recent advancements in text-guided audio generation have yielded promising results in diverse domains, including sound effects, speech, and music. However, jointly generating speech with environmental audio remains challenging due to the inherent disparities in their acoustic patterns and temporal dynamics. We propose ImmersiveTTS, an environment-aware text-to-speech (TTS) model that generates natural speech seamlessly integrated within environmental contexts by explicitly modeling cross-modal interactions. Our model builds on a multimodal diffusion transformer and fuses transcript-aligned speech latent with text-conditioned environmental context via joint attention. To enhance semantic consistency, we introduce a domain-specific representation alignment objective tailored to environment-aware TTS, leveraging complementary self-supervised representations from speech and audio encoders. Experimental results show that ImmersiveTTS achieves higher naturalness, intelligibility, and audio fidelity than existing approaches across objective metrics and human listening tests.

</details>


### [65] [AnchorSteer: Self-Discovered Concept Injection for Structure-Preserving Music Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31053)
*Chih-Heng Chang, Keng-Seng Ho, Chih-Yu Tsai, Kuan-Lin Chen, Yi-Hsuan Yang, Jian-Jiun Ding*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 值得关注 (72.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AnchorSteer 提出一种自发现概念注入框架，通过结构锚定与自监督语义解耦，在严格保持节奏和旋律结构的同时实现可控音乐编辑，在 ZoME-Bench 和主观测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控音乐编辑面临语义-结构纠缠问题：现有编辑方法在修改高层属性时往往破坏节奏和旋律结构，而结构保持方法又会抑制语义响应能力。需要一种既能实现语义变换又能严格保持音乐结构的方法。

Method: 提出 AnchorSteer 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自发现语义注入——通过自监督重建目标从扩散模型内部表征中提取可解释的、无标签的概念向量，实现属性解耦；(2) 结构锚定——使用结构适配器在编辑过程中强制保持节奏和旋律一致性。提供无条件和有条件两种注入变体以平衡鲁棒性和语义强度。

Result: 在 ZoME-Bench 和主观测试中，AnchorSteer 在实现显著语义变换的同时保持了高保真结构，优于纯语义注入和纯结构锚定的基线方法。

Conclusion: AnchorSteer 通过解耦语义编辑与结构保持，有效解决了可控音乐编辑中的语义-结构纠缠问题，为音乐编辑提供了一种无需标注数据即可实现属性控制的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究可控音乐编辑（音频生成/编辑），属于多模态生成方向下的音频生成范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on music editing, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: music editing, structure preservation, diffusion model, concept injection, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Controllable music editing is to modify high-level attributes while strictly preserving rhythmic and melodic structures. However, this task is challenged by a semantic-structural entanglement: steering methods often degrade structure to achieve editing performance, while structural adaptors suppress semantic responsiveness. We propose AnchorSteer, a framework that disentangles this tension by coupling structural anchoring with self-discovered semantic steering. The proposed approach probes internal representations to extract interpretable, label-free concept vectors via a self-supervised reconstruction objective, isolating attributes without curated data. During editing, these portable, plug-and-play concept vectors are injected into diffusion hidden manifolds while a structural adaptor enforces consistency. Variants for unconditioned and conditioned injections are provided to balance robustness and semantic strength. Experiments on ZoME-Bench and subjective tests show that the proposed framework outperforms both steering-only and anchoring-only baselines, enabling significant semantic transformations with high-fidelity structural preservation.

</details>

### [66] [FBHM: Functional Benchmarking and Steering of VLMs for Hateful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31349)
*Paramananda Bhaskar, Naquee Rizwan, Daksh Jogchand, Saurabh Kumar Pandey, Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出FBHM，一个基于功能分类的仇恨模因基准数据集，揭示VLM在标准数据集上高准确率但在FBHM上近乎随机表现的严重泛化差距。同时提出LSV（可学习转向向量），一种超低数据量的因果干预方法，仅需500个样本即可提升约30个Macro-F1点，优于上下文学习和PEFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仇恨模因检测基准存在结构性缺陷——混淆了修辞机制与目标社区特征，无法对模型脆弱性进行因果评估。需要构建一个解耦的、基于功能分类的基准来系统评估VLM的真实多模态推理能力。

Method: 1) 构建FBHM基准：沿两个正交轴（25种修辞功能 × 10个目标社区）系统构建5000个模因。2) 提出LSV（可学习转向向量）：在模型中间层插入可学习的转向向量，通过因果干预目标优化，仅需500个样本（50个独特基础模因）即可高效提升检测性能。

Result: SOTA VLM在FBHM上性能从标准数据集的~90%骤降至接近随机水平。LSV在仅500个样本上训练，将FBHM性能提升约30个Macro-F1点，且不降低源域性能，优于上下文学习和LoRA等PEFT方法。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在仇恨模因检测中依赖数据集特定启发式而非鲁棒的多模态推理，存在严重泛化差距。LSV提供了一种高效、低成本的因果干预方案来弥合这一差距，为多模态安全检测提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在仇恨模因检测中的安全漏洞与鲁棒性，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴。FBHM基准测试系统性地评估VLM在面对恶意内容时的脆弱性，LSV方法通过因果干预提升模型安全性，与[多模态安全与攻防]主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on hateful meme detection and VLM safety, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of continuous models, generation, and world models. Signals: VLM safety, hateful meme detection, functional benchmarking, causal intervention, low-data steering

Abstract: Hateful meme detection remains a formidable challenge for vision-language models, as existing benchmarks are structurally observational - confounding rhetorical hate mechanisms with target community features and preventing causal evaluation of model vulnerabilities. To address this, we introduce FBHM, a systematically curated benchmark of Functionality Based Hateful Memes constructed along two orthogonal axes: 25 distinct rhetorical functionalities and 10 target communities (5,000 memes total). Benchmarking state-of-the-art VLMs reveals a severe generalization gap: models highly accurate on standard datasets catastrophically drop to near-random performance on FBHM, proving they exploit dataset-specific heuristics rather than robust multimodal reasoning. To efficiently close this gap, we propose LSV (learnable steering vectors), an ultra-low data regime strategy that applies a causal intervention objective on as few as 500 steering samples (50 unique base memes), boosting FBHM performance by ~30 Macro-F1 points while outperforming in-context learning and PEFT without degrading source-domain performance.

</details>

*Jun Wang, Xiaohao Xu, Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TouchSafeBench基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在人机协作场景中的碰撞接地能力（判断是否正在碰撞或即将碰撞）。在Habitat 3.0中构建2940个模拟场景，测试多种VLM和视觉表征，发现当前最佳模型Macro-F1仍低于50%，表明视觉流畅性不等于物理可问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全人机协作需要监控系统不仅能够描述场景，还能判断机器人是否与场景或人发生碰撞或即将碰撞。现有VLM缺乏这种"碰撞接地"能力，即无法将视觉观察与机器人几何、视角、场景布局、人体接近度和时序运动绑定来推断接触状态。

Method: 在Habitat 3.0模拟器中构建TouchSafeBench基准，包含2940个室内共处场景（社交导航和社交重排），提供多视角RGB-D观测、俯视轨迹图、相机元数据和模拟器接触标签。定义两个任务：当前安全状态分类和即将碰撞预警。评估多种前沿/机器人VLM及九种视觉表征。

Result: 最佳模型平均Macro-F1低于50%；显式深度信息并未自动转化为机器人身体碰撞证据；机器人-场景接触比人-接触风险更难判断。当前VLM在碰撞接地任务上远未达到可靠水平。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型的视觉流畅性不等于物理可问责性。可靠的机器人安全监控需要显式绑定视角、机器人形态、度量几何和未来碰撞的表征。TouchSafeBench揭示了具身VLM的核心局限性。

Classification reason: 论文研究VLM在机器人安全人机协作中的碰撞接地能力（collision grounding），属于多模态安全评测范畴。TouchSafeBench基准测试评估VLM对碰撞状态的分类与预警能力，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）中的安全评测方向高度吻合。同时涉及世界模型中的具身环境建模，因此也匹配world_model方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM safety for robotics, which is outside your configured priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: VLM safety benchmark, collision grounding, human-robot collaboration, low model performance, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Safe human--robot collaboration requires more than visual description: a monitor must determine whether the robot body is safely separated, already colliding with the scene or a person, or about to collide. We call this capability collision grounding: binding visual observations to robot body geometry, camera viewpoint, scene layout, human proximity, and temporal motion in order to infer present and imminent contact. We introduce TouchSafeBench, a physics-grounded benchmark for evaluating collision grounding in vision-language models (VLMs). Built in Habitat~3.0, TouchSafeBench contains 2,940 simulated indoor co-presence episodes across social navigation and social rearrangement, with synchronized multi-view RGB-D observations, top-down trajectory maps, calibrated camera metadata, and simulator-derived contact labels. We study two deployment-facing tasks: classifying the current safety state and warning about imminent collision before contact. Across three frontier or robotics-oriented VLMs and nine visual representations, current models remain far from reliable: the best average Macro-F1 stays below 50\%, explicit depth is not automatically transformed into robot-body collision evidence, and robot--scene contact is consistently harder than human-contact risk. TouchSafeBench reveals a central limitation of embodied VLMs: visual fluency does not imply physical accountability. Reliable robot safety monitors will need representations that explicitly bind viewpoint, robot morphology, metric geometry, and future collision. We will release the benchmark upon acceptance.

</details>
