<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在开放世界部署中存在"语义傲慢"问题：由于缺乏显式的负知识，模型会将未知异常强制拟合到已知类别并给出高置信度预测。传统开放集识别方法依赖被动密度估计或像素级异常生成，效率低且效果有限。

Method: 借鉴生物免疫负选择原理，利用LLM的生成推理能力主动"幻觉"出"语义抗体"——即近分布异常（如相似外观、上下文异常）的文本描述，这些抗体在VLM的高维潜空间中有效约束已知类别的决策边界，实现对未知异常的检测与拒识。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和四个具有挑战性的OOD基准数据集上，Immuno-VLM取得了新的SOTA性能，显著优于传统开放集识别方法。

Conclusion: Immuno-VLM通过生物启发的免疫负选择机制与LLM生成推理的结合，有效解决了VLM开放世界可信赖性悖论，为多模态模型的安全可靠部署提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注VLM在开放世界部署中的可信赖性问题，提出利用LLM生成"语义抗体"来检测未知异常（开放集识别），属于多模态安全与防御范畴，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel bio-inspired OOD detection for VLM trustworthiness, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: Open-world trustworthiness, Semantic antibodies, VLM safety, OOD detection, Negative selection

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models have achieved unprecedented success in zero-shot recognition by aligning visual features with broad semantic concepts. However, this semantic abstraction creates a critical vulnerability in open-world deployment: the ``Hubris of Semantics'', where models force-fit unknown anomalies into known categories with high confidence due to the lack of explicit negative knowledge. To address this \textit{Open-World Trustworthiness Paradox}, we propose \textbf{Immuno-VLM}, a bio-inspired framework that adapts the biological principle of \textbf{Immunological Negative Selection} to high-dimensional latent spaces. Departing from traditional Open-Set Recognition methods that rely on passive density estimation or inefficient pixel-space outlier generation, Immuno-VLM leverages the generative reasoning of Large Language Models to actively hallucinate ``Semantic Antibodies'', textual descriptions of near-distribution outliers (e.g., look-alikes, contextual anomalies) that effectively bound the decision space of known experiments on ImageNet-1K and four challenging OOD benchmarks reveal that Immuno-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [69] [COMPASS: Cognitive MCTS-Guided Process Alignment for Safe Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30838)
*Wenkai Shen, Pengyang Zhou, Jiahe Xu, Jiaming Qian, Haozhe He, Zhihao Huang, Chaochao Chen, Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COMPASS 提出了一种基于认知MCTS的过程对齐框架，通过认知树探索（CTE）高效合成隐蔽攻击轨迹，并结合内省式逐步对齐（ISA）对中间动作进行细粒度安全监督，在保证搜索代理实用性的同时实现鲁棒的安全对齐，且所需训练数据大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的搜索代理具备多步推理和工具使用能力，但检索过程会引入安全退化——有害意图可能分解为看似无害的子查询，最终导致不安全结果。现有对齐方法难以捕获稀疏的安全信号，也无法监督多步交互中的多样化违规行为。

Method: 提出COMPASS框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 认知树探索（CTE），利用MCTS高效合成隐蔽攻击轨迹，探索有害查询的多种分解路径；(2) 内省式逐步对齐（ISA），对中间动作进行细粒度过程监督，隔离风险行为并实现安全对齐。

Result: 实验表明COMPASS在安全-效用权衡上表现优异，同时所需训练数据大幅减少。

Conclusion: COMPASS通过认知MCTS引导的过程对齐，有效解决了搜索代理中检索诱导的安全退化问题，实现了鲁棒的安全对齐与良好的实用性保持。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM驱动的搜索代理的安全对齐问题，涉及对抗攻击（stealthy attack trajectories）与安全防御（process alignment），属于多模态安全与攻防范畴。虽然论文聚焦于文本搜索代理而非多模态，但其核心方法论（MCTS引导的对抗攻击生成与过程对齐）与多模态安全攻防中的越狱攻击、对抗防御高度相关，且该方向涵盖广义的LLM/多模态安全对齐。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent safety alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: safety alignment, search agents, MCTS, process supervision, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: LLM-powered search agents enable multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, these capabilities introduce retrieval-induced safety degradation, as harmful intents may decompose into seemingly innocuous sub-queries that lead to unsafe outcomes. Existing alignment methods struggle to capture sparse safety signals and fail to supervise diverse violations across multi-step interactions. We propose COMPASS, a Cognitive MCTS-Guided Process Alignment framework designed to achieve robust safety alignment throughout the agent workflow while preserving general utility. COMPASS integrates cognitive tree exploration (CTE) to efficiently synthesize stealthy attack trajectories, and introspective step-wise alignment (ISA) to isolate risky intermediate actions for fine-grained process supervision. Empirical results show that COMPASS achieves a favorable safety-utility trade-off while requiring substantially less training data.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出状态空间约束仿真框架，在生成模型上实例化多种合成认知画像（persona），通过结构化流形表示不同人类视角，进行多元对齐评估；并分析这些模拟评估者在序列推理和随机提示扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 现代生成架构能以较高一致性实例化和维持评估性人格；但在序列推理和随机扰动下，人格一致性出现系统性退化，表现为状态空间漂移和语义不一致。

Conclusion: 静态对齐约束不足以维持鲁棒的评估行为，需要在生成系统中嵌入动态、自适应的调节机制来保持连贯的认知仿真，为更适应人类多样性的AI评估方法奠定基础。

Signals: alignment evaluation, persona-based framework, state-space drift, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Current alignment paradigms for generative artificial intelligence rely predominantly on monolithic benchmarking frameworks that reduce the plurality of human judgment to aggregated statistical baselines, thereby obscuring cultural, demographic, and contextual variability in evaluation. We introduce a state-space constrained emulation framework for AI evaluation that replaces singular assessment functions with a structured manifold of synthetic cognitive profiles representing diverse human perspectives. We show that modern generative architectures can instantiate and maintain these evaluative personas with high consistency, enabling a form of pluralistic, perspective-dependent benchmarking that more closely reflects real-world consensus variability. However, we further analyze the stability of these simulated evaluators under sequential inference and stochastic prompt perturbations, revealing systematic degradation in persona coherence that manifests as state-space drift and semantic inconsistency. These findings suggest that static alignment constraints are insufficient for sustaining robust evaluative behavior over time. Instead, we argue for the necessity of embedding dynamic, viability-driven regulatory mechanisms within generative systems to preserve coherent cognitive emulation. By framing persona-based evaluation as a structured dynamical system over latent representation manifolds, this study provides a foundation for more adaptive, human-aligned, and context-sensitive approaches to AI evaluation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构化的多层级视觉扰动框架，系统分析VLA（视觉-语言-动作）驾驶模型中视觉信息对驾驶行为的影响，揭示了视觉-行为依赖在不同评估协议（开环轨迹预测 vs. 闭环安全评估）下呈现不同模式，且视觉信息在不同抽象层级的利用不均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶模型在自动驾驶中展现出潜力，但其驾驶行为如何依赖于视觉信息尚不清楚。当前的评估协议主要关注聚合性能指标，缺乏结构化的诊断方法来量化视觉-行为依赖关系。

Method: 提出一个多层级视觉扰动框架，沿三个互补维度组织受控视觉扰动：通道级退化（channel-level degradation）、信息级破坏（information-level disruption）和结构级修改（structure-level modification）。在开环轨迹预测和交互式闭环安全评估两种协议下测试VLA驾驶系统的行为响应。

Result: 实验揭示了评估依赖的依赖模式：不同评估协议下视觉-行为依赖关系表现不同；视觉信息在不同抽象层级的利用不均匀，某些层级扰动对行为影响更大。

Conclusion: 这些发现呼吁对VLA驾驶模型进行更结构化的分析和更原则性的设计，以更好地理解视觉信息如何塑造驾驶行为，并开发更安全、更鲁棒的自动驾驶系统。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA驾驶模型中视觉信息对行为决策的影响，通过多层级视觉扰动框架分析视觉-行为依赖关系，属于多模态系统的鲁棒性与安全性分析，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）中对抗攻击、安全评测方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Analyzes visual grounding in VLA driving models, but the subtopic is outside your priority areas and lacks direct connection to your core interests. Signals: VLA driving model, visual perturbation framework, open-loop vs closed-loop evaluation, visual-behavior dependency, autonomous driving safety

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising capability in autonomous driving, highlighting the potential of unified multimodal architectures for jointly modeling perception and planning. However, how current VLA-based driving behavior is grounded in visual information remains poorly understood. Existing evaluation protocols mainly focus on aggregate performance metrics, lacking structured and practical diagnostics to quantify visual-behavior dependency. In this work, we introduce a structured multi-level visual perturbation framework to analyze visual-behavior dependency in VLA-based driving models systematically. The framework organizes controlled visual perturbations along three complementary dimensions: channellevel degradation, information-level disruption, and structurelevel modification. We apply it to VLA-based driving systems and evaluate behavioral responses under both open-loop trajectory prediction and interactive closed-loop safety evaluation. Experimental results reveal evaluation-dependent dependency patterns and uneven visual grounding across abstraction levels. These findings call for more structured analyses and principled design of VLA driving models to better understand how visual information shapes behavior and develop safer, more robust systems.

</details>


### [72] [Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31556)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet, Simon Henniger, Mahzarin R. Banaji*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CY, cs.HC

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现，经过对齐训练的VLM在面对性别模糊的输入图像时，内部编码的女性关联信号在生成阶段被系统性抑制，而男性信号被放大，导致输出偏向男性。作者提出LALS（潜关联倾斜分数）指标，通过将视觉token激活投影到文本嵌入空间来逐层测量概念关联，揭示了内部表征与输出之间的解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法在性别清晰可见时能有效避免VLM表达人口统计偏见，但对实践中常见的模糊输入（如全副武装的工人、背对镜头的人物）研究甚少。作者希望探究VLM在模糊输入下是否仍存在性别偏见，以及模型内部表征与最终输出之间是否存在差异。

Method: 提出LALS（Latent Association Leaning Score），一种零样本度量方法，将视觉token激活投影到模型的文本嵌入空间，逐token、逐层测量概念关联强度。在15个职业、800+张性别模糊图像上对4种VLM进行实验，通过最小提示压力暴露职业-性别默认关联，并进行逐层分析和颜色消融实验。

Result: 内部表征与输出系统性解耦：模型内部编码了女性关联，但输出却偏向男性。逐层分析发现不对称过滤机制——男性信号从输入到输出端到端放大，而女性信号在中间层达到峰值后在被生成前被抑制。颜色消融实验表明，服装颜色等文化负载视觉线索会进一步调节内部关联。

Conclusion: VLM在模糊输入下存在系统性性别偏见，即使内部编码了女性关联，输出仍偏向男性。这表明当前对齐方法在模糊场景下存在盲区，需要更细粒度的内部表征监控与干预策略来确保公平性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在模糊输入下对女性表征的抑制问题，属于多模态模型中的偏见、公平性与安全相关议题，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）中涉及的对抗攻击、安全评测方向高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on gender bias in VLM alignment, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of continuous models, generation, and efficiency. Signals: VLM bias, ambiguous input, internal representation decoupling, LALS metric, alignment side effects

Abstract: Alignment teaches vision-language models (VLMs) to avoid expressing demographic biases, and when gender is clearly visible they largely succeed. Far less is known about ambiguous inputs (a worker in full gear, a figure seen from behind) cases common in practice yet rarely studied. We find that minimal prompting pressure exposes occupation-gender defaults when prompting ambiguous input images, with models collapsing to male even for strongly female-stereotyped occupations. But do these outputs reflect what models actually encode internally? We introduce LALS (Latent Association Leaning Score), a zero-shot metric that projects visual-token activations into the model's text-embedding space to measure concept associations per token and layer. Across 15 occupations, over 800 gender-ambiguous images, and four VLMs, internal representations and outputs are systematically decoupled: models often encode a female association internally yet output male. Layer-wise analysis reveals an asymmetric filter -- male signal amplifies end-to-end while female signal peaks mid-network and is suppressed before generation -- and a color ablation shows that culturally loaded visual cues such as clothing color further modulate these internal associations.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文系统探究了LVLM架构设计（语言基础、视觉表征、语义对齐三个维度）对三种幻觉（共现、相似性、不确定性）的影响，提出了细粒度幻觉基准CoSimUE，实验发现模型参数规模对减少幻觉效果有限，而联合增强视觉保真度和对齐质量最为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM幻觉问题严重制约其可靠性，现有工作多关注改进内部组件，但缺乏从架构设计层面系统理解幻觉成因的研究。本文旨在揭示架构设计选择与幻觉鲁棒性之间的映射关系。

Method: 将架构设计分解为语言基础(LF)、视觉表征(VR)和语义对齐(SA)三个维度，将幻觉分为共现型、相似型和不确定型三类。提出CoSimUE基准，通过受控文本扰动和随机扰动创建细粒度幻觉场景，在7个设计方面进行实验分析。

Result: 1) 模型参数规模对减少三类幻觉效果有限；2) 更大更好的语言基础可减少共现幻觉；3) 更强的视觉编码器和更高分辨率缓解相似性错误；4) 有效的对齐策略减轻不确定性幻觉；5) 联合增强视觉保真度和对齐质量带来最全面的改进。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统建立了架构设计与幻觉鲁棒性之间的联系，为开发可靠高效的LVLM提供了实用指导，指出单纯扩大参数规模并非缓解幻觉的有效途径，视觉表征与对齐质量的联合优化更为关键。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究LVLM幻觉与架构设计的关系，属于多模态幻觉缓解方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性" 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Systematic architectural analysis of LVLM hallucination is valuable but not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: LVLM hallucination, architectural factors, CoSimUE benchmark, cross-dimensional analysis, limited scaling impact

Abstract: Hallucination remains one of the key challenges undermining the reliability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). But what makes an LVLM hallucinate less? Many existing efforts focus on improving internal components of the model. We argue that hallucination fundamentally stems from how the model architecture is designed. To investigate this, we factor the architecture design into three dimensions: Linguistic Foundation (LF), Visual Representation (VR), and Semantic Alignment (SA), and categorize hallucinations into Co-occurrence, Similarity, and previously overlooked Uncertainty types. Building on this formulation, we propose CoSimUE, a benchmark that creates fine-grained hallucination scenarios through controlled textual perturbations and random perturbations, enabling mapping between design choices and hallucination behaviors. Experiments across 7 design aspects show that: 1) the widely emphasized scaling of model parameters has only limited impact on reducing all three types of hallucinations; 2) larger and better-trained language foundations can reduce co-occurrence hallucinations; 3) stronger visual encoders and higher resolutions mitigate similarity errors; 4) effective alignment strategies alleviate uncertainty hallucinations. 5) Furthermore, cross-dimensional analysis reveals that jointly enhancing visual fidelity and alignment quality yields the most comprehensive improvements. This study provides the first systematic exploration linking architecture-level design to hallucination robustness, offering practical guidance for developing reliable and efficient LVLMs.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO)，通过将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中，解决视觉偏好 DPO 中因配分函数不匹配导致的理论不一致问题；同时引入 Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist) 正则化器以及对比样本编辑策略生成硬负样本，在五个基准上取得最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1) IC-VCO：将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中，确保偏好优化目标的数学严谨性；2) VCDist：辅助可靠性门控正则化器，促进多图像对比训练与单图像推理之间的一致性；3) 对比样本编辑策略：通过精确语义扰动生成硬负样本。

Result: 在五个基准上，IC-VCO 取得最佳综合性能，对比样本编辑策略的有效性得到验证。

Conclusion: IC-VCO 通过理论严谨的视觉对比优化和硬负样本生成，有效缓解了多模态幻觉，为视觉偏好优化提供了更可靠的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于缓解多模态幻觉问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination），因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses multimodal hallucination via visual contrastive optimization, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal hallucination, Visual preference DPO, In-context contrastive optimization, Hard negative generation, VLM reliability

Abstract: Multimodal hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Standard textual Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) often fails to mitigate it due to a lack of explicit visual supervision. While existing works introduce visual preference DPO by contrasting original images against negative ones, they suffer from a theoretically inconsistent objective caused by partition function mismatches and rely on coarse-grained negatives that could enable shortcut learning. In this work, we propose In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO). By placing contrastive images within a shared multi-image context, IC-VCO ensures a mathematically rigorous objective. We further introduce Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist), an auxiliary reliability-gated regularizer that encourages consistency between multi-image contrastive training and single-image inference. Finally, we propose a contrastive sample editing strategy that generates hard negatives via precise semantic perturbations. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate IC-VCO's best overall performance and the effectiveness of our sample editing strategy. Code and data are available at .

</details>


### [75] [YARD: Y-Architecture Register Decoding for Efficient Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31429)
*Ting Chen, Geng Li, Guohao Chen, Yu Hu, Guan Huang, Mai Chen, Langsheng Lei, Jun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (59.68) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: YARD提出了一种无需训练的Y-Architecture Register Decoding框架，通过在中间解码层分支并用register tokens替换patch-level视觉token来构建视觉退化分支，从而提供可靠的对比解码信号以缓解LVLM幻觉，同时避免二次前向传播带来的高延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free的对比解码方法存在两个问题：完全丢弃视觉token过于极端，会引发语言幻觉；而破坏输入图像的方式对视觉证据的控制粗糙且需要两次完整前向传播，推理延迟高。因此需要一种更高效且有效的幻觉缓解方法。

Method: YARD基于"可靠的文本到视觉对齐主要出现在中间解码层"这一观察，设计Y型架构：共享浅层计算，在关键中间层分支。退化分支将patch-level视觉token替换为register tokens（保留全局语义但缺乏细粒度局部证据），提供图像感知但局部欠对齐的对比信号；主分支正常推理。这种设计避免了第二次完整前向传播。

Result: 在生成式和判别式幻觉基准上，YARD在多个LVLM上一致达到最先进的幻觉缓解效果，同时显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: YARD通过巧妙的Y型架构设计，在不增加训练开销的前提下有效缓解了LVLM幻觉，并大幅提升了推理效率，为训练-free的对比解码方法提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LVLM中的幻觉缓解问题，提出Y-Architecture Register Decoding框架，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性方向，直接匹配[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Addresses hallucination in LVLMs with efficient decoding, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous/multimodal models or generation. Signals: training-free contrastive decoding, Y-architecture for efficiency, hallucination mitigation, register tokens, no secondary forward pass

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) seeks to mitigate hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of a standard model and a visually degraded model. However, existing training-free CD methods suffer from sub-optimal degraded branches: completely dropping visual tokens is too extreme and induces language hallucinations, while corrupting input images offers coarse control over visual evidence and suffers from high inference latency due to requiring two full forward passes. To address these dilemmas, we propose YARD, a training-free Y-Architecture Register Decoding framework. Motivated by the observation that reliable text-to-vision grounding predominantly emerges in the middle decoder layers, YARD constructs the degraded branch internally by sharing shallow-layer computations and branching exactly at this critical stage. For the degraded branch, YARD replaces patch-level visual tokens with register tokens, which preserve global image semantics but lack fine-grained local evidence. This image-aware yet locally under-grounded design provides a faithful contrastive signal without extreme modality mismatch, while the Y-architecture strictly avoids a costly second forward pass. Extensive experiments on generative and discriminative hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that YARD consistently achieves state-of-the-art hallucination mitigation across multiple LVLMs, alongside a significant reduction in inference latency.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语作为全球第六大语言，尚无系统性的大语言模型幻觉评测工作，现有评测多聚焦于高资源语言，低资源语言的幻觉问题缺乏关注。

Result: BenHalluScore 在模型和任务间范围为7.72%-55.42%，揭示了模型间幻觉校准的显著差异；思维链提示作为缓解策略仅改变了响应分布，未能持续改善幻觉判别能力。

Conclusion: BenHalluEval 建立了首个孟加拉语专用幻觉基准，揭示了单轨评测和仅提示策略在低资源语言场景中的不足。

Importance reason: Focuses on Bengali hallucination evaluation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Bengali-specific, hallucination evaluation, low-resource language, no continuous language model, no video generation

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at .

</details>


### [77] [Fighting Numerical Hallucinations via Data-centric Compilation for Online Financial QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31064)
*Hao Chen, Xing Tang, Qirui Liu, Weijie Shi, Shiwei Li, Fuyuan Lyu, Weihong Luo, Xiku Du, Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 本文提出数据为中心的推理编译器DCRC，通过对抗数据构建、多阶段训练和编译执行推理三阶段，解决金融QA中RAG系统的数值推理幻觉问题，在离线基准和在线系统中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在金融QA中存在噪声敏感、计算脆弱和可审计性危机三大挑战，而模型中心的方法（单独优化检索器或生成器）无法系统性解决这些问题。

Method: 提出DCRC框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 对抗数据构建——合成含可控噪声的训练样本；(2) 多阶段训练——训练数据为中心的结构化智能体(DSA)，具备显式证据审计和程序合成能力；(3) 编译执行推理——DSA将用户查询和检索文档转化为可验证、可执行的推理程序。

Result: 在离线金融QA基准和真实在线金融问答系统中均取得了优于基线方法的性能，有效缓解了数值推理幻觉。

Conclusion: 数据为中心的范式能够系统性解决RAG在金融QA中的数值推理幻觉问题，通过显式证据审计和可执行程序推理保证了推理的忠实性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于金融QA中的数值推理幻觉问题，属于多模态/语言模型幻觉缓解与可靠性提升范畴，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）这一CANONICAL子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial QA numerical hallucination, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: financial QA, numerical hallucination, data-centric compilation, RAG robustness, online system deployment

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced online data services, particularly in the domain of financial question answering (FinQA). However, such systems remain susceptible to numerical reasoning hallucinations, which critically undermine reliability in high-stakes financial applications. Although retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has been widely adopted to ground responses in external knowledge, it introduces three persistent challenges: noise sensitivity, calculation fragility, and an auditability crisis. Existing model-centric approaches, which primarily focus on optimizing either the retriever or generator in isolation, still struggle to address these issues in an integrated manner. In this work, we pioneer a data-centric paradigm and propose a novel framework, the Data-centric Reasoning Compiler (DCRC). The framework operates through three cohesive phases: (1) adversarial data construction, which synthesizes training examples with controlled noise to teach robustness; (2) multi-stage training that cultivates a Data-centric Structuring Agent (DSA) capable of explicit evidence auditing and program synthesis; and (3) a compile-and-execute inference process, where the DSA transforms user queries and retrieved documents into verifiable, executable reasoning programs. This data-driven framework ensures faithful numerical reasoning by design. We conduct extensive experiments on established offline benchmarks and further validate our framework through deployment in a real-world online financial QA system.

</details>

### [78] [UniScale: Adaptive Unified Inference Scaling via Online Joint Optimization of Model Routing and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30898)
*Kaiyu Huang, Xingyu Wang, Mingze Kong, Zhubo Shi, Yuqian Hou, Hong Xu, Zhongxiang Dai, Minchen Yu, Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (64.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出UniScale，一个将模型路由（model routing）和测试时扩展（test-time scaling）统一优化的在线推理框架。通过将问题建模为上下文多臂老虎机并使用LinUCB学习推理策略，UniScale在动态推理场景中实现了更细粒度的质量-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将模型路由和测试时扩展作为两个独立维度处理，导致模型路由只能提供粗粒度的离散性能变化，而单模型TTS存在容量上限且收益递减。两者分离限制了动态推理环境中的适应性。

Method: 提出统一推理扩展（UIS）框架，将模型路由和TTS统一到单一优化空间中。基于此，设计UniScale在线框架，将自适应UIS建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，使用LinUCB算法学习推理策略，并引入效率感知学习和成本建模以在高维动作空间中实现稳定可扩展的优化。

Result: 实验表明，UniScale能够有效利用UIS空间的协同效应，在多样化的动态推理场景中实现更细粒度且持续更优的质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 统一模型路由与测试时扩展的联合优化框架能够克服两者分离的局限性，在线学习策略可以有效适应动态推理环境，实现更优的推理效率与质量平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM推理时模型路由与测试时扩展的联合优化，属于多模态/语言模型推理效率与对齐优化范畴。虽然论文主要针对语言模型，但其提出的统一推理扩展框架（UIS）和在线学习策略（LinUCB）可推广到多模态场景，与"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）中关于推理策略优化、偏好学习的思想高度契合。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel unified optimization of model routing and test-time scaling for efficient LLM inference, aligning with model efficiency priorities. Signals: efficient inference, model routing, test-time scaling, online learning, quality-cost trade-off

Abstract: In real-world deployments of large language models (LLMs), balancing inference quality and computational cost has become a central challenge. Existing approaches tackle this trade-off along two largely independent dimensions: model routing, which switches among models of different scales to match request complexity, and test-time scaling (TTS), which adjusts inference-time compute within a fixed model for fine-grained control. However, this decoupled design introduces inherent limitations. Model routing yields coarse-grained, discrete performance changes due to the sparse set of model scales, while single-model TTS often encounters capacity ceilings and exhibits diminishing returns as compute increases. Moreover, treating the two mechanisms separately restricts adaptability in dynamic inference environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Unified Inference Scaling (UIS), which unifies model routing and TTS in a single optimization space. Building on this formulation, we propose UniScale, an online framework that models adaptive UIS as a contextual multi-armed bandit problem and learns inference policies via LinUCB. The framework incorporates efficiency-aware learning and cost modeling to ensure stable and scalable optimization over high-dimensional action spaces. Evaluation shows that UniScale effectively exploits the synergy in the UIS space to deliver a fine-grained and consistently better quality-cost trade-off across diverse, dynamic inference scenarios.

</details>


### [79] [Attend to Evidence: Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention Supervision for Multimodal RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30912)
*Ruina Hu, Chen Wang, Lai Wei, Jionghao Bai, Bin Yu, Weiran Huang, Kai Wang, Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 EASE（Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention），一种为多模态 RLVR 引入视觉证据过程监督的方法。EASE 将标注的视觉证据区域转换为平滑的视觉 token 目标，在 RL 训练中引导响应到图像的注意力，仅在高奖励轨迹上生效。标注仅作为特权训练标签，推理时无需额外输入。在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，相比 DAPO 平均提升 2.5-3.1 分，涵盖感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RLVR 方法仅基于最终答案的结果奖励进行优化，无法区分基于视觉证据的正确回答与依赖语言先验捷径或运气猜测的回答。对于需要视觉定位的问题，这种结果奖励无法提供细粒度的过程监督，导致模型可能忽略关键图像区域。

Method: EASE 方法包含三个关键步骤：(1) 将人工标注的证据区域（bounding box）转换为平滑的视觉 token 注意力目标；(2) 在 RL 训练中，仅对高奖励轨迹施加响应到图像的注意力引导损失，使模型关注与答案相关的视觉区域；(3) 标注仅在训练阶段作为特权信号使用，推理时仅需原始图像和问题。该方法可集成到 DAPO 等 RLVR 框架中。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，EASE 相比 DAPO 基线在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理等基准上平均提升 2.5-3.1 分。诊断实验和消融研究表明，EASE 能更好地将视觉注意力与标注的证据区域对齐。

Conclusion: EASE 通过引入视觉证据过程监督，有效弥补了 RLVR 中结果奖励的不足，提升了 VLM 在需要视觉定位任务上的表现。该方法简单有效，且推理时无额外开销，为多模态 RL 对齐提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EASE 方法，在 RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）框架中引入视觉证据过程监督，通过空间注意力引导提升 VLM 的视觉定位与推理能力。核心是多模态强化学习与对齐，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel attention supervision method for multimodal RLVR, but the subtopic is not in the user's priority list and lacks direct connection to continuous models or generation. Signals: Multimodal RLVR, Visual evidence supervision, Attention alignment, Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves vision-language models (VLMs) by optimizing outcome rewards derived from final answers. However, such outcome-only rewards do not tell the model which image regions justify an answer. For questions that require visual grounding, these rewards cannot distinguish responses supported by relevant visual evidence from those produced by language-prior shortcuts or lucky guesses. We introduce EASE (Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention), which augments multimodal RLVR with visual-evidence process supervision. EASE converts annotated evidence regions into a smoothed visual-token target and uses it to guide response-to-image attention during RL training, but only on high-reward trajectories. The annotations are used solely as privileged training labels, while inference requires only the original image and question. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-4B, and Qwen3-VL-8B, EASE raises average scores over DAPO by 2.5 to 3.1 points on perception, hallucination, visual math, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Diagnostics and ablations show that EASE better aligns visual attention with annotated evidence regions.

</details>

*Xiaobo Wang, Tong Wu, Min Tang, Jiaqi Li, Qi Liu, Zilong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 提出 SAVE 框架，利用价值函数作为自适应锚点，将在线策略响应转化为监督信号，通过对比学习目标对奖励模型进行自监督改进，在六个基准上取得一致领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建强奖励模型（RM）用于语言模型对齐时，获取多样且可靠的偏好数据成本高昂且困难，且随着策略模型不断演化，静态 RM 训练难以跟上策略变化，导致 RM 与策略分布不匹配。

Method: SAVE 框架利用价值函数（value function）作为提示特定的自适应锚点，对在线策略生成的响应进行评分作为反馈；计算 RM 优势值并过滤模糊样本，通过对比学习目标（contrastive objective）更新奖励模型，实现自监督的在线策略 RM 训练。

Result: 在六个多样化基准上进行了严格实证评估，SAVE 在所有数据集上均取得优于基线的方法，并在三种 RL 算法（GRPO、RLOO、GSPO）和不同策略骨干网络上保持一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: SAVE 框架通过价值锚定的在线策略反馈有效解决了 RM 训练中的分布偏移和数据瓶颈问题，为奖励模型的自监督改进提供了一种通用且有效的方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SAVE 框架，通过价值函数锚定的在线策略反馈来改进奖励模型（RM）的自监督训练，核心是 RLHF 中的奖励模型优化与对齐。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度相关，尽管论文主要针对语言模型，但其方法（RLHF、奖励模型、偏好学习）是该子主题的核心内容。

Importance reason: Focuses on RLHF reward model improvement, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: RLHF, reward model, on-policy feedback, self-supervised, value function

Abstract: Building strong reward models (RMs) for language model alignment is bottlenecked by the cost and difficulty of acquiring diverse and reliable preference data from human annotation or judge models. It is dramatically worse as the policy evolves beyond the static RM training. Therefore, we propose SAVE (Self-supervised reward model improvement via Value-Anchored On-policy feedback), a framework that grades on-policy responses as feedback by using the value function for on-policy RM training. SAVE naturally converts the reward-graded on-policy responses into supervision with a prompt-specific value head as an adaptive anchor. It computes RM advantages and filters ambiguous samples to update the RM via a contrastive objective. The effectiveness of SAVE for enhancing RM training is strongly validated through rigorous empirical evaluation across six diverse benchmarks. It achieves outperforming results across all datasets while maintaining consistent improvements across three RL algorithms (GRPO, RLOO, GSPO) and different policy backbones.

</details>


### [81] [SCOPE: Self-Play via Co-Evolving Policies for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31433)
*Wai-Chung Kwan, Aryo Pradipta Gema, Joshua Ong Jun Leang, Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SCOPE提出了一种数据自由的自我对弈框架，通过协同进化两个策略（Challenger生成文档基础任务，Solver通过多轮检索回答）来训练语言模型，并使用冻结的初始模型作为自评判器。在多个7-8B模型上，SCOPE在开放任务上提升高达+10.4分，在保留的短问答任务上提升高达+13.8分，超越了使用约9K精心设计提示的GRPO_data。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我对弈方法需要规则可检查的答案，而开放任务依赖于精心设计的提示或前沿模型作为评判者，这限制了自我对弈在开放任务中的应用。作者希望设计一种无需外部监督、无需人工标注数据、也无需依赖强大外部评判模型的自我对弈框架，使语言模型能够在开放任务中自我提升。

Method: SCOPE框架包含两个协同进化的策略：Challenger从文档中生成基于文档的开放任务，Solver通过多轮检索回答这些任务。使用冻结的初始模型副本作为自评判器，该评判器从源文档中编写任务特定的评分标准，并据此对Solver的回答进行评分。Challenger和Solver交替更新，Challenger被鼓励生成Solver当前能力边界附近的任务（保持任务难度在Solver的"前沿"），从而持续推动Solver进步。

Result: 在Qwen2.5、Qwen3、OLMo-3三个7-8B指令微调模型上，SCOPE在8个开放任务基准上提升高达+10.4分，匹配或超越使用约9K精心设计提示训练的GRPO_data。在7个保留的短问答基准上，SCOPE提升高达+13.8分，在所有三个模型上均超越GRPO_data。消融实验表明：协同进化Challenger是必要的；收益来自检索和合成两方面的改进，但相对贡献因任务而异；评分标准生成质量是自评判的瓶颈。

Conclusion: SCOPE证明了无需外部监督、无需人工数据、无需强大外部评判模型，仅通过自我对弈即可在开放任务上显著提升语言模型性能，并且这种提升可以泛化到保留的短问答任务上。协同进化Challenger是保持任务难度在Solver前沿的关键。评分标准生成质量是当前自评判机制的主要瓶颈，未来工作可聚焦于此。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SCOPE框架，通过自我对弈（self-play）协同进化两个策略（Challenger和Solver）来训练语言模型，无需外部监督。虽然论文主要聚焦于语言模型，但其核心思想——通过自我博弈进行无监督对齐与能力提升——与多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）方向高度相关，且该方向涵盖RLHF、偏好学习等对齐方法。SCOPE使用自评判（self-judge）机制替代外部奖励模型，属于对齐与偏好学习范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on self-play for open-ended tasks, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: self-play, co-evolving policies, open-ended tasks, data-free, language model training

Abstract: Self-play can train language models without external supervision. However, existing methods require rule-checkable answers, leaving open-ended tasks dependent on curated prompts or frontier-model judges. We introduce SCOPE, a data-free self-play framework for open-ended tasks that co-evolves two policies: a Challenger that generates document-grounded tasks, and a Solver that answers them through multi-turn retrieval. A frozen copy of the initial model serves as the self-judge, which writes task-specific rubrics from the source document and grades Solver responses against them. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, OLMo-3), SCOPE improves open-ended performance by up to +10.4 points on eight benchmarks and matches or exceeds GRPO_data trained on ~9K curated prompts. Although trained only on open-ended tasks, SCOPE also improves held-out short-form QA by up to +13.8 points on seven held-out benchmarks, surpassing GRPO_data on all three models. Ablations show that co-evolving the Challenger is necessary to keep tasks near the Solver's frontier, that gains arise from improvements in both retrieval and synthesis with the relative contribution varying by task, and that rubric generation quality is the bottleneck for self-judging.

</details>


### [82] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo, Hoyoung Kim, Minwoo Jang, Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.DC, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedVPA-GP，一种联邦变分偏好对齐框架，通过 Gumbel-Softmax 先验和联邦混合先验解决联邦学习中数据稀缺导致的个性化偏好学习后验坍塌问题，并引入正交损失显式分离偏好原型，在 HH-RLHF 数据集上优于传统单一奖励模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习对齐框架通常强制使用单一奖励模型，平均化了用户间冲突的偏好（如 helpfulness vs. harmlessness）。变分偏好学习（VPL）虽能实现个性化，但在去中心化场景中面临后验坍塌问题——本地数据稀缺和异构性导致推理退化。因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能解耦多样化用户偏好的联邦对齐方法。

Method: 提出 FedVPA-GP 框架：(1) 联邦混合先验（Federated Mixture Prior），使客户端利用聚合的全局分布作为动态先验，稳定变分推理；(2) Gumbel-Softmax 先验，用于离散偏好原型的可微采样；(3) 正交损失（Orthogonal Loss），显式强制偏好原型在潜空间中分离，避免偏好混淆。

Result: 在 HH-RLHF 数据集上的实验表明，FedVPA-GP 显著优于单一奖励模型基线，成功解耦了冲突的用户意图，并支持动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP 通过联邦混合先验和正交损失有效解决了联邦场景下个性化偏好对齐的后验坍塌问题，为隐私保护下的多用户偏好解耦提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究联邦学习中基于变分偏好学习（VPL）的个性化LLM对齐，属于多模态/语言模型对齐范畴。虽然论文主要聚焦语言模型，但其偏好对齐方法（RLHF/DPO范式）与多模态对齐方向高度相关，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Focuses on federated preference alignment for LLMs, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Federated learning, preference alignment, posterior collapse, Gumbel-Softmax, orthogonal loss

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>

## 多模态推理与思维链 [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Diversity Matters: Revisiting Test-Time Compute in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30713)
*Yijie Tong, Yifan Hou, Shaobo Cui, Antoine Bosselut, Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (60.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）中的测试时计算（TTC）策略，发现特征启发式方法无效，多数投票在单模型场景下增益有限，原因是预测多样性不足。为此提出基于熵的TTC方法（ETTC），在模型集成场景下利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型，理论上证明其优于多数投票，实验表明小模型可协同增强大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时计算（TTC）在纯语言模型中已被证明能有效提升推理能力，但在视觉语言模型（VLM）中的应用和收益尚未被充分探索。本文旨在系统研究TTC在VLM中的效果，并解决现有方法在VLM场景下效果有限的问题。

Method: 首先在7个VLM和6个基准上系统评估了基于特征的评分和多数投票两种TTC策略。理论分析表明预测多样性不足是多数投票效果有限的原因。提出基于熵的TTC（ETTC），通过计算预测熵选择最置信的预测：单模型时退化为多数投票，模型集成时利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型。给出了ETTC优于多数投票的理论证明。

Result: 特征启发式方法在VLM中失败；多数投票在单模型场景下仅带来微小提升；多模型集成能提供更丰富的多样性，但标准多数投票无法处理模型能力差异。ETTC在模型集成场景下一致优于多数投票和最佳单模型，且小模型可以协同增强大模型，实现标准策略无法达到的集成增益。

Conclusion: 多样性是VLM中测试时计算有效性的关键因素。基于熵的ETTC方法能有效利用模型集成中的置信度差异，实现优于多数投票的性能。该工作揭示了VLM中TTC的局限性，并为多模型协同推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究视觉语言模型中的测试时计算（TTC）策略，属于多模态推理中推理时计算扩展（inference scaling）方向，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Systematic study of test-time compute in VLMs with theoretical analysis, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM test-time compute, entropy-based ensemble, prediction diversity analysis, multi-model synergy, no continuous/video focus

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) strategies have emerged as a lightweight approach to boost reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, their application and benefits for vision-language models (VLMs) remain underexplored. We present a systematic study of TTC across seven VLMs and six benchmarks, specifically analyzing feature-based scoring and majority voting methods. We find that feature heuristics fail and voting yields only modest gains in single-model settings. We theoretically show that this limitation stems from a lack of prediction diversity: when outputs are highly correlated, voting provides little benefit. In contrast, multi-model ensembles offer richer diversity, yet standard majority voting fails to account for varying model capabilities. To address this, we propose Entropy-based TTC (ETTC), which selects the most confident prediction based on predictive entropy. Our method reduces to majority voting in the single-model case, but in model ensembles, it leverages confidence disparities to prioritize stronger models. We prove that ETTC outperforms majority voting under mild assumptions and empirically demonstrate that it consistently surpasses both voting and the best individual model. Crucially, our results show that smaller models can synergistically enhance larger ones, unlocking ensembling gains not achievable with standard strategies.

</details>


### [84] [Seeing Isn't Knowing: Do VLMs Know When Not to Answer Spatial Questions (and Why)?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30557)
*Yue Zhang, Zun Wang, Han Lin, Yonatan Bitton, Idan Szpektor, Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (59.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建 SpatialUncertain 评测框架，系统研究 VLM 在空间推理中面对遮挡和视角歧义时的表现，发现模型普遍存在过度自信回答的倾向（遮挡下准确率约30%，视角歧义下低于10%），且难以识别哪些额外视角能提供可靠证据，呼吁超越答案正确性、评估模型何时应拒绝回答的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准通常假设视觉观察是充分且可靠的，只关注模型能否给出正确答案，而忽略了模型是否能够识别问题不可回答的情况。然而真实环境中遮挡和视角歧义普遍存在，模型需要具备"知道何时不回答"的能力。

Method: 构建 SpatialUncertain 控制评测框架，设计两类观测挑战：(1) 遮挡——隐藏目标信息；(2) 视角歧义——产生误导性视觉线索。对每种配置设计在干净观测下可回答但在挑战下需拒绝回答的空间问题，并评估模型能否识别哪些额外视角可解决视角歧义。

Result: 在多个前沿开源和闭源 VLM 上，模型在遮挡下平均准确率约30%，在视角歧义下低于10%，且存在过度自信回答的倾向。即使提供额外视角，部分模型在识别可靠证据方面接近随机水平。

Conclusion: VLM 在空间推理中缺乏对自身知识边界的认知，容易在证据不足时过度自信回答。研究呼吁评测应超越答案正确性，关注模型是否知道何时应拒绝回答以及如何寻求可靠证据。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 在空间推理任务中面对遮挡和视角歧义时的表现，核心关注模型是否知道何时应拒绝回答以及如何寻求可靠证据，属于多模态推理（空间推理）范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for VLM spatial reasoning limitations, but only tangentially touches continuous multimodal models or world models. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning, occlusion and perspective ambiguity, abstention evaluation, overconfidence in VLMs, SpatialUncertain benchmark

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for vision-language models (VLMs) deployed in real-world environments. However, visual observations are inherently limited representations of a 3D world: occlusion can render objects invisible, and perspective can make geometric properties misleading. Despite this, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks typically assume that observations are sufficient and reliable, focusing on whether models produce correct answers rather than whether they recognize when a question cannot be answered and what additional observations would be needed. In this work, we challenge this assumption by constructing a controlled evaluation framework, SpatialUncertain, and introducing two types of observation challenges: (1) occlusion, which hides target information, and (2) perspective ambiguity, which produces misleading visual cues. For each configuration, we design spatial questions that are answerable under clean observations but require abstention under the introduced challenges. We further evaluate whether models can identify which additional viewpoints would resolve perspective ambiguity. Our results across a diverse set of frontier open- and closed-source VLMs reveal two consistent failure modes. First, models are prone to overconfident answering, attempting to solve spatial reasoning tasks even when visual evidence is incomplete or misleading, with average accuracy around 30\% under occlusion and below 10\% under perspective ambiguity. Second, even when additional views are available, some models perform near random chance in identifying which would provide reliable evidence. Together, our findings call for moving beyond answer correctness toward evaluating whether models know when to abstain and how to seek reliable evidence.

</details>


### [85] [Your Teacher Can't Help You Here: Combating Supervision Fidelity Decay in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30833)
*Yanjiang Liu, Jie Lou, Xinyan Guan, Yuqiu Ji, Hongyu Lin, Ben He, Xianpei Han, Le Sun, Xing Yu, Yaojie Lu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现在线策略蒸馏中存在监督保真度衰减（SFD）问题：随着学生生成前缀变长，教师模型的下一token分布变得不自信且缺乏区分度，导致反向KL蒸馏中的纠正信号减弱。为此提出Lookahead Group Reward（LGR），通过评估学生候选token在下一步诱导的教师置信度来分配组归一化奖励，并设计熵触发树注意力机制保持效率。在6个数学和代码基准上，LGR在7B学生模型上平均提升mean@8达2.57分，长序列场景下提升更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（On-Policy Distillation, OPD）通过让学生在其自身生成轨迹上接受教师token级反馈来迁移推理能力，但该方法存在一个关键瓶颈：随着学生生成序列变长，教师对下一token的预测分布变得不自信且区分度下降，导致反向KL蒸馏中的纠正信号减弱，学生模型在长推理链上出现漂移累积。本文旨在解决这一监督保真度衰减问题。

Method: 提出Lookahead Group Reward（LGR），核心洞察是：下一步的教师置信度反映了未来反向KL监督的区分强度。LGR评估学生当前步的top-K候选token，通过计算每个候选token在下一步诱导的教师置信度来分配组归一化奖励。同时设计熵触发树注意力机制（entropy-triggered tree-attention），仅在教师分布熵较高时展开候选分支，以维持计算效率。

Result: 在6个数学和代码基准上，LGR在7B学生模型上相比OPD平均提升mean@8达2.57分。在长序列生成场景下增益更大，AIME-26基准上在39k token长度时提升达4.92分。

Conclusion: 本文识别并解决了在线策略蒸馏中的监督保真度衰减问题，提出的Lookahead Group Reward通过前瞻性评估教师置信度有效缓解了长链推理中的学生漂移，实验证明该方法在数学和代码推理任务上显著优于基线，且长序列场景下优势更为突出。

Classification reason: 该论文研究在线策略蒸馏中教师监督保真度衰减问题，提出Lookahead Group Reward方法提升长链推理能力，在数学和代码基准上验证。虽然论文本身聚焦于LLM推理蒸馏，但其核心关注长链推理（long reasoning chains）与推理能力迁移，与多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）中"推理时计算扩展"、"数学/代码推理"等主题高度契合，且该方向涵盖纯文本推理场景。

Importance reason: Focuses on distillation fidelity decay in LLM reasoning, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: on-policy distillation, supervision fidelity decay, reverse-KL, lookahead reward, math/code reasoning

Abstract: On-policy distillation transfers reasoning capabilities by training a student model on its own generated trajectories using token-level feedback from a teacher. However, we identify a critical bottleneck, \textbf{Supervision Fidelity Decay (SFD)}: as student-generated prefixes lengthen, the teacher's next-token distribution becomes less confident and less discriminative. Consequently, the teacher-dependent corrective signal in reverse-KL distillation weakens, causing student drift to compound across long reasoning chains. To mitigate SFD, we introduce \textbf{Lookahead Group Reward (\ours{})}. Building on the insight that next-step teacher confidence reflects the discriminative strength of future reverse-KL supervision, \ours{} evaluates the student's top-K candidate tokens by the teacher confidence they induce at the subsequent step and assigns a group-normalized reward. To maintain computational efficiency, we further design an entropy-triggered tree-attention mechanism. Across six math and code benchmarks, \ours{} improves mean@8 by \textbf{2.57} points over OPD for a 7B student, with gains increasing in longer-generation and reaching +\textbf{4.92} points on AIME-26 at 39k tokens.

</details>


### [86] [iVGR: Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning for MLLMs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31096)
*Chang-Bin Zhang, Yujie Zhong, Qiang Zhang, Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (88.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 iVGR 框架，通过强化学习将视觉定位能力内化到 MLLM 的文本推理过程中，使模型在推理时无需显式输出目标框即可实现精准的视觉定位推理，在细粒度评测上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉定位 CoT 方法在推理时强制要求模型输出显式目标框，反而会干扰答案预测，导致性能下降。作者假设视觉定位能力可以被内化到文本推理过程中，无需显式定位即可实现更好的推理效果。

Method: 提出 iVGR 框架，采用双流训练策略：一个文本推理流（textual stream）与一个高质量视觉定位流（visually grounded stream）通过一致性奖励进行对齐，利用强化学习将定位能力迁移到文本推理过程中，使模型在推理时无需显式定位框即可准确推理。

Result: 在多个细粒度评测基准上，iVGR 显著优于现有基线方法，同时保持了支持工具辅助推理工作流的灵活性。

Conclusion: 视觉定位能力可以通过强化学习内化到文本推理过程中，无需显式定位框即可实现更好的视觉定位推理，为 MLLM 的细粒度感知推理提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 的视觉定位推理与 Chain-of-Thought，属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances multimodal reasoning via RL internalization, aligning with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: RL for internalized visual grounding, consistency reward for dual-stream training, outperforms explicit grounding baselines, supports tool-assisted inference, relevant to continuous VLM and multimodal latent reasoning

Abstract: While visually grounded Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has emerged as a promising paradigm to enhance fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), its efficacy during the inference phase remains underexplored. In this work, we empirically find that mandating explicit object boxes in visually grounded CoT during inference often degrades performance compared to standard textual CoT, which reasons without explicit visual grounding. We hypothesize that the visual localization capability can be internalized into the textual CoT and that the mandatory explicit grounding introduces unnecessary interference with the model's primary objective of answer prediction. To address this problem, we propose Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning (\textbf{iVGR}), a novel reinforcement learning framework that transfers localization capabilities into the textual reasoning process. We employ a dual-stream training strategy, where a textual stream is aligned with a high-quality visually grounded stream via a proposed consistency reward, enabling the model to localize accurately without explicit grounding during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines on fine-grained benchmarks, while maintaining the flexibility to support tool-assisted inference workflows.

</details>


### [87] [LARK: Learnability-Grounded Trajectory Selection for Efficient Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30651)
*Tianrun Yu, Kaixiang Zhao, Chih-Chun Chen, Amanda Hughes, Taylor W. Killian, Fenglong Ma, Weitong Zhang, Porter Jenkins*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LARK 提出了一种基于可学习性的推理轨迹选择方法，通过定义可学习性因子 ρ 并引入 χ² 正则化选择策略，在保持数据分布覆盖的同时选择学生模型能高效学习的轨迹，从而提升推理蒸馏效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法多基于轨迹质量或模型置信度等启发式标准，忽略了轨迹对学生模型是否可学习这一关键因素，导致蒸馏效率低下。

Method: 提出 LARK 框架，核心包括：(1) 可学习性因子 ρ，刻画学生模型训练损失下降速率；(2) 可学习性代理（learnability proxy）用于高效估计 ρ；(3) χ² 正则化选择策略，在可学习性与分布覆盖之间取得平衡，并具有理论保证。

Result: 在多个基座模型和推理任务上，LARK 一致优于现有数据选择基线。诊断分析表明 LARK 分数能预测下游训练效用，且 LARK 选择的轨迹能加速监督微调损失下降。

Conclusion: LARK 验证了可学习性引导的轨迹选择在推理蒸馏中的有效性，为数据高效蒸馏提供了新视角和理论支撑。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法，属于多模态/大语言模型推理能力蒸馏与优化的范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）紧密相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on reasoning distillation efficiency, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: reasoning distillation, trajectory selection, learnability factor, χ² regularization, supervised fine-tuning

Abstract: We study trajectory selection for reasoning distillation, where teacher-generated reasoning trajectories are selectively used as supervision for a student model. Existing methods rely on heuristics such as trajectory quality or model confidence, but they often overlook whether a trajectory is learnable by the student. In this paper, we present LARK, a learnability-grounded method for reasoning trajectory selection. LARK selects trajectories that the student can learn efficiently while preserving the generalization of the full training distribution. At the core of LARK is a learnability factor $\rho$, which characterizes the rate at which the student's training loss decreases. To estimate this rate efficiently and maintain generalization, we introduce a learnability proxy and a $\chi^2$-regularized selection policy that balances learnability and distributional coverage, both with strong theoretical guarantees on their estimation error. Empirically, LARK consistently outperforms data selection baselines across multiple base models and reasoning tasks. Diagnostic analyses show that the LARK score predicts downstream training utility and that LARK-selected trajectories induce faster supervised fine-tuning loss reduction. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [88] [The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404)
*Xudong Zhang, Jian Yang, Shengkai Wang, Jiangpeng Tian, Shaowen Chen, Xian Wei, Ke Li, Xiong You*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (66.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双干预框架，系统研究LLM在导航规划中处理文本化空间表征时的语言归纳偏差。实验发现：拓扑信息是鲁棒规划的坚实"盾牌"；语言格式是把双刃剑，效果取决于模型大小和压缩程度；语义信息是致命弱点，错误语义会系统性破坏规划过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的导航系统通常将空间信息（拓扑图、语义栅格图）转化为文本描述作为输入，但文本化空间表征的语言结构及其包含的上下文特征（拓扑、几何、语义）对LLM行为的影响尚未被系统研究。这些设计常被视为中性的工程决策，而非影响LLM性能的关键因素。

Method: 提出双干预框架：(1) 表征干预——改变语言格式（自然语言 vs 结构化格式）和语言压缩程度，观察对导航规划的影响；(2) 上下文干预——结合上下文特征组合与冲突探测，明确LLM处理不同上下文线索时的偏好与弱点。在多种空间推理任务和多个模型规模上进行实验。

Result: 拓扑信息是鲁棒规划的坚实"盾牌"和支柱；语言格式是双刃剑，效果取决于模型大小、任务需求和压缩程度；语义信息是致命弱点——错误的语义线索会系统性破坏规划过程。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的导航系统中，有效的文本化空间表征应保持拓扑完整性、根据模型能力校准表征压缩程度、确保语义正确性，而非简单采用单一表征格式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM在导航规划中的空间推理能力，属于多模态推理范畴（空间推理是视觉推理的重要子方向），因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"。

Importance reason: Strong linguistic bias analysis for spatial reasoning, but only tangentially touches continuous multimodal models and lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics. Signals: Linguistic inductive bias, spatial reasoning, navigation planning, topological vs semantic cues, model scale effects

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based navigation systems commonly construct explicit spatial representations (e.g., topological graphs, semantic raster maps) and translate them into textual descriptions as LLMs' inputs. However, the linguistic structures of such text-based spatial representations and the choices of contextual features (e.g., topology, geometry) they contain are often treated as neutral engineering decisions rather than key factors that shape LLMs' behavior. To fill the gap, we propose a dual-interventional framework that disentangles linguistic structures from different contextual cues to evaluate the linguistic inductive bias of LLMs for navigation planning. In the framework, representation intervention varies the linguistic format and the degree of linguistic compression, clarifying when linguistic representations support or inhibit navigation planning. Context intervention, combined with contextual feature combination and conflict probing, explicitly clarifies the preferences and weaknesses of LLMs when processing different contextual cues. Experiments across diverse spatial reasoning tasks and multiple model scales reveal a consistent pattern: topological information is a sturdy shield and the backbone of robust planning; linguistic format is a double-edged sword whose effect depends on model size, task demands, and the compression level; and semantic information is a fatal Achilles' heel -- incorrect semantic cues can systematically derail the planning process. Overall, our study shows that effective text-based spatial representations in LLM-based navigation should preserve topological integrity, calibrate representational compression to model capacity, and ensure semantic correctness, rather than simply adopting a single representation. Our code is publicly available at .

</details>

### [89] [Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30968)
*WenZhang Wei, Zhipeng Gui, Dehua Peng, Tiandi Ye, Huayi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (64.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出变分适配器 VACSR，将图文匹配中因细粒度语义标注稀缺导致的二值分类问题重新建模为变分推理问题，通过构建跨模态相似度的潜空间并引入正则化缓解过拟合，在图像-文本检索、域泛化和 base-to-novel 泛化任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数图文匹配或多类图像分类数据集缺乏细粒度的跨模态匹配标注，迫使连续的相似度空间被压缩为二值分类边界，导致假负样本并损害跨模态任务的泛化性能。现有方法仅建模模态内歧义性，忽略了标注本身的缺陷，导致不确定性分配次优。

Method: 提出 Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation (VACSR)，将细粒度语义稀缺下的图文匹配重新表述为变分推理问题。构建跨模态相似度的潜空间，利用正则化技术缓解对二值标注的过拟合，从而更合理地分配不确定性。

Result: 在图像-文本检索、域泛化和 base-to-novel 泛化三个任务上进行了实验，结果表明 VACSR 有效提升了跨模态相似度表征的质量和泛化能力。

Conclusion: VACSR 通过变分推理框架有效解决了细粒度语义标注稀缺导致的二值分类过拟合问题，为跨模态相似度表征提供了一种鲁棒的泛化方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图文匹配与跨模态相似度表征，核心任务是图像-文本检索（image-text retrieval），属于多模态检索范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）完全匹配，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on cross-modal retrieval with variational inference, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: cross-modal retrieval, variational inference, image-text matching, domain generalization, base-to-novel generalization

Abstract: The core of vision-language models lies in measuring cross-modal similarity within a unified representation space. However, most image-text matching or multi-class image classification datasets lack fine-grained cross-modal matching annotations, forcing the continuous similarity space into binary classification boundaries. This compression induces false negative samples and significantly impairs the generalization performance of cross-modal tasks. While prior research has attempted to mitigate this by modeling intra-modal ambiguity, it often overlooks inherent annotation flaws, leading to suboptimal uncertainty allocation. To address these challenges, we propose a Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation (VACSR). This approach reformulates image-text matching with fine-grained semantic scarcity as a variational inference problem. It constructs a latent space for cross-modal similarity and uses regularization techniques to mitigate overfitting to binary annotations. Experiments on image-text retrieval, domain generalization, and base-to-novel generalization demonstrate the proposed method's effectiveness and robust generalization ability.

</details>


### [90] [Learning Whom to Trust: Market-Feedback Adaptive Retrieval for Frozen LLMs in Event-Driven Financial RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31201)
*Zijie Zhao, Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (40.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种市场反馈自适应的金融RAG方法，通过贝叶斯源记忆（Bayesian Source Memory）根据已实现的残差收益反馈动态调整检索来源权重，在冻结LLM的情况下显著提升了事件驱动金融预测的F1分数和下游投资组合夏普比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融RAG中，传统方法仅按文本相关性排序检索结果，但金融市场的有用证据来源取决于事件类型、预测周期和市场环境。作者希望让检索层能够根据市场反馈自适应调整，学习"在何时信任哪个信息来源"。

Method: 提出Frozen Reader with Source Memory框架：1) 对每个公司-新闻锚点，检索相关金融新闻和SEC文件段落；2) 附加决策前市场背景卡片；3) 使用冻结的LLM作为阅读器；4) 通过外部贝叶斯源记忆（Bayesian Source Memory）根据已实现的残差收益反馈更新检索来源的信任权重，实现检索层的自适应调整。

Result: 在FinRL-DeepSeek/FNSPID任务的89只纳斯达克股票数据集上，Frozen Reader with Source Memory相比无记忆基线将宏F1从0.438提升至0.471，下游投资组合夏普比率从0.52提升至0.84。监督LoRA微调阅读器虽能提升静态RAG，但不如冻结阅读器+源记忆方法。

Conclusion: 对于金融RAG，"学习从何处检索"与"学习如何阅读"同等重要。所提出的市场反馈自适应检索方法提供了一种简单、模块化的途径来适应市场动态，且无需更新LLM本身。

Classification reason: 该论文研究金融领域的检索增强生成（RAG），核心贡献在于通过市场反馈自适应调整检索层（Bayesian Source Memory），属于多模态/跨模态检索的范畴。虽然论文聚焦金融文本，但其"学习从何处检索"的思想与多模态检索中的自适应检索策略高度相关，因此复用[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on financial RAG with market feedback, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and is flagged by negative keywords. Signals: Financial RAG, Bayesian source memory, event-driven prediction, frozen LLM, market feedback adaptation

Abstract: Financial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rank evidence by textual relevance, but in financial markets the useful evidence source depends on event type, forecast horizon, and market context. We study news-triggered event-impact prediction as a point-in-time financial RAG problem. For each company-news anchor, the system retrieves related financial news and SEC filing passages, appends a pre-decision market-context card, and predicts multi-horizon residual-return signals. Our method keeps the large language model (LLM) reader frozen and adapts the retrieval layer through an external Bayesian source memory updated from matured residual-return feedback. On a fixed 89-stock Nasdaq-oriented universe derived from the FinRL-DeepSeek/FNSPID task, using original FNSPID news and point-in-time EDGAR filing passages, Frozen Reader with Source Memory improves held-out macro-F1 from 0.438 to 0.471 and downstream portfolio Sharpe from 0.52 to 0.84 relative to Frozen Reader with No Memory. A supervised LoRA reader improves static RAG modestly, but does not improve over the frozen source-memory reader. These results suggest that, for financial RAG, learning where to retrieve from can be as important as learning how to read, offering a simple, modular route to market-feedback adaptation.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Classification: Dynamic Adapter Routing for Continual Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31229)
*Alicja Dobrzeniecka, Filip Szatkowski, Sebastian Cygert, Szymon Lukasik, Bartlomiej Twardowski*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出持续多模态检索（CMR）的新评测框架，发现标准类增量学习方法在此场景下效果有限，并提出基于原型路由的动态适配器路由（DAR）方法，通过选择适配器并加权组合实现更优的持续检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的持续学习研究主要聚焦于分类任务（类增量学习），而检索任务中的持续更新问题被严重忽视。标准 CIL 方法在检索场景下可能不适用，需要专门针对持续多模态检索的评测框架和方法。

Method: 提出 Dynamic Adapter Routing (DAR) 方法：基于原型（prototype）路由选择适配器，并通过模型集成方式组合多个适配器的输出，实现持续多模态检索中的知识保留与迁移。

Result: 在提出的 CMR 评测框架下，DAR 方法优于现有基线方法，并在分布外（OOD）评测中展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 持续多模态检索具有独特的挑战，标准类增量学习方法无法带来有意义的提升；DAR 方法通过动态适配器路由有效应对这些挑战，为该方向提供了新的研究思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究持续多模态检索（continual multimodal retrieval），属于多模态检索范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on continual multimodal retrieval, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in generation and world models. Signals: Continual multimodal retrieval, dynamic adapter routing, prototype-based routing, out-of-distribution generalization, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: While retrieval is a core function of vision-language models, continually updating these models for retrieval tasks remains critically underexplored. Existing work often approaches continual retrieval through the lens of class-incremental learning (CIL), evaluating both standard CIL methods and retrieval-oriented adaptations in settings that may not fully capture the retrieval-specific dynamics. To address this, we introduce a new, principled evaluation framework for continual multimodal retrieval (CMR) spanning diverse visual domains, and systematically evaluate common approaches within this setting. Our empirical analysis shows that standard CIL methods fail to yield meaningful gains in our more challenging scenario. Therefore, we propose Dynamic Adapter Routing (DAR), a novel approach based on adapters selected through prototype-based routing and combined via model achieves superior performance over the previous baselines and demonstrates strong generalization under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results highlights the unique challenges of CMR and encourages further research in this direction.

</details>


### [92] [Inference-Free Multimodal Learned Sparse Retrieval for Production-Scale Visual Document Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30917)
*Gyu-Hwung Cho, Youngjune Lee, Kiyoon Jeong, Siyoung Lee, Sanggyu Han, Hervé Dejean, Stéphane Clinchant, Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出V-SPLADE，一个面向大规模视觉文档检索的推理时免神经编码的稀疏检索器。通过引入标题门控token监督（caption-gated token supervision），利用VLM生成的标题作为词汇线索激活检索相关词汇维度，解决了视觉稀疏表示难以捕获文档图像中词汇内容的词汇接地问题。在六个基准上NDCG@5平均提升+13.8pp，在1870万文档语料上R@5提升超过两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法要么使用VLM密集或多向量模型（检索质量好但推理时需要神经查询编码），要么使用基于OCR或标题的BM25（避免查询编码但需要耗时的文本提取或生成）。缺少一种可部署的、能对视觉文档进行词汇索引、推理时无需神经编码的检索系统。

Method: 提出V-SPLADE，一种推理时免编码的稀疏检索器。核心创新是标题门控token监督（caption-gated token supervision）：在训练时利用VLM生成的标题作为词汇线索，通过门控机制激活检索相关的词汇维度，使视觉稀疏表示能够更好地捕获文档图像中的词汇内容。该方法在训练时使用标题监督，推理时无需任何神经编码。

Result: 在六个视觉文档检索基准上，V-SPLADE的NDCG@5平均比同规模密集基线高+13.8pp，比基于OCR或标题的BM25基线高+6.3pp。在1870万文档的大规模语料上，R@5比同规模密集基线提升超过两倍，通过分数融合进一步使竞争检索器R@5提升+2.4pp。

Conclusion: V-SPLADE成功填补了视觉文档检索中推理时免神经编码的空白，证明了推理时免编码的稀疏检索可以达到密集级别的效果，为大规模视觉文档检索提供了可部署的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉文档检索，提出V-SPLADE稀疏检索器，属于跨模态/多模态文档检索范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on retrieval efficiency for visual documents, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: inference-free sparse retrieval, visual document search, caption-gated token supervision, production-scale, not autoregressive or continuous model

Abstract: As large-scale visual-document corpora such as arXiv papers and enterprise PDFs continue to grow, visual-document retrieval has gained increasing attention; yet it still lacks a deployable system that lexically indexes visual documents to serve queries without neural encoding at scale. Existing methods either achieve strong retrieval quality with VLM-based dense or multi-vector models but require neural query encoding at serving time, or avoid query encoding with OCR- or caption-based BM25 at the cost of time-consuming text extraction or generation. To fill this missing serving regime, we present V-SPLADE, an inference-free sparse retriever for visual-document retrieval. However, such inference-free multimodal learned sparse retrieval systems remain underexplored and have not yet shown dense-level effectiveness under high sparsity. We attribute this limitation to a lexical grounding problem: visual sparse representations often fail to capture the lexical content embedded in document images. To address this problem, we introduce caption-gated token supervision, a training-only signal that uses VLM-generated captions as lexical cues to activate retrieval-relevant vocabulary dimensions. With this supervision, V-SPLADE improves average NDCG@5 across six visual-document retrieval benchmarks by +13.8pp over the same-scale dense baseline and by up to +6.3pp over OCR- or caption-based BM25 baselines. On an 18.7M-document corpus, it more than doubles R@5 over the same-scale dense baseline and further improves competing retrievers through score fusion by up to +2.4pp R@5. Code will be released soon at .

</details>

### [93] [TeachObs: A Human-Validated Benchmark for Multimodal Teaching Observation and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30673)
*Yeil Jeong, Youngjin Yoo, Seobin Sohn, Hyejin Han, Jinseo Lee, Scott Howard, Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TeachObs 是一个人工验证的多模态课堂教学观察基准，包含30个公开课程视频（5158个15秒片段），由7位研究者标注39个二值观察编码（20个视觉+19个非视觉），并构建了基于信度和流行度的金标签。同时有3位专家对30节课进行课程级评分。基于该基准评估了5个前沿视觉LLM在三个赛道上的表现，发现没有单一模型在所有赛道中一致最优，且添加中间帧会同时增加正确和错误归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂视频中包含丰富的可观察教学行为，但其教学和视觉信号很少以适合模型评估的形式组织。现有基准缺乏对课堂教学多模态信号的细粒度标注和系统化评估，因此需要一个人工验证的基准来评估AI系统在课堂视频分析中的能力。

Method: 收集30个来自8个国家的公开课程视频，分割为5158个15秒片段。7位研究者对每个片段标注39个二值观察编码（20个视觉编码如手势、板书、指向、视觉材料；19个非视觉编码如指令、监控、提问、反馈、反思）。基于Krippendorff's alpha构建信度和流行度感知的金标签。3位专家对30节课进行课程级评分（教学设计、教学传递、学习者反应、学习材料、课程收尾）。评估5个视觉LLM在三个赛道：纯文本片段编码、文本+帧片段编码、课程级覆盖评估（LLM-as-judge协议）。

Result: 没有单一模型在所有三个赛道中一致最优；添加中间帧会同时增加每个场景的正确和错误归因；模型评估相对于专家评分者高估了程序清晰的课程。

Conclusion: TeachObs 支持细粒度标注基准测试和整课评估，展示了AI系统在课堂视频分析中可以辅助哪些方面，以及哪些方面仍需专家判断。该基准覆盖了不同学科、课堂形式和标注难度，为多模态教学观察提供了有价值的评估平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向课堂教学视频的多模态评测基准 TeachObs，包含细粒度标注和课程级评估，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on classroom observation benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: multimodal benchmark, classroom video, teaching observation, visual LLM evaluation, not continuous token or generation

Abstract: Classroom videos contain observable teaching practices, but their pedagogical and visual signals are rarely organized in forms suitable for model evaluation. We present \textit{TeachObs}, a human-validated benchmark for multimodal teaching observation in classroom videos. \textit{TeachObs} includes 30 public lesson videos from eight countries divided into 5,158 fixed 15-second scenes. Seven researchers annotated each scene with 39 binary observation codes, covering 20 visual codes, such as gesture, board work, pointing, and visual materials, and 19 nonvisual codes, such as instruction, monitoring, questioning, feedback, and reflection. Gold segment labels are constructed using reliability- and prevalence-aware rules based on Krippendorff's alpha. In addition to segment-level labels, three expert raters produced lesson-level ratings and qualitative evaluations of instructional design, instructional delivery, learner response, learning materials, and lesson closure across the 30 lessons, with rater coverage detailed in the body. Using these two human reference layers, we evaluate five vision-capable frontier LLMs across three tracks - text-only segment coding, text + frame segment coding, and lesson-level coverage scored under an LLM-as-judge protocol - and find that no single model consistently outperforms others across all three tracks, that adding a mid-frame inflates both true and false attributions per scene, and that model evaluations over-rate procedurally clear lessons relative to expert raters. \textit{TeachObs} therefore supports both fine-grained annotation benchmarking and whole-lesson evaluation, showing where AI systems can assist classroom video analysis and where expert judgment remains necessary across varied subjects, classroom formats, and annotation difficulty levels.

</details>


### [94] [BilliardPhys-Bench: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning and Visual Dynamics of Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30900)
*Ben Wang, Xiaogang Li, Ruochen Gao, Peiyao Xiao, Chengliang Xu, Zeyu Wang, Zichao Chen, Bing Zhao, Hu Wei*

Main category: physics.app-ph, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BilliardPhys-Bench，一个基于合成台球环境的物理推理基准，用于评估多模态大模型在单张图像中预测物体运动、碰撞和最终位置的能力。实验发现模型存在"停滞偏差"——当物理结果难以推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型在静态图像识别上表现良好，但在直观物理推理方面仍然薄弱。现有基准缺乏对物体运动与交互的细粒度评估，因此需要专门针对物理推理能力的评测基准。

Method: 设计了一个程序化生成的台球物理环境，包含摩擦和弹性碰撞。基准测试三个能力：球间碰撞预测、墙壁反弹推理、运动停止后最终位置估计。评估了 GPT、Claude、Gemini、Qwen 系列的最新 MLLM。

Result: 模型性能随模拟时间增长和场景几何复杂度增加而下降。发现了一致的失败模式"停滞偏差"：当正确物理结果难以推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互。

Conclusion: 当前 MLLM 在视觉动力学推理上存在明显不足，需要更好的物理归纳偏置来改进多模态架构。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 BilliardPhys-Bench，一个用于评估多模态大模型物理推理能力的基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时，该基准关注物理世界中的物体运动预测与交互推理，也与世界模型方向（world_model）相关。

Importance reason: Benchmark reveals stasis bias in MLLMs but is a physics benchmark, not directly advancing the user's priority subtopics. Signals: physical reasoning benchmark, stasis bias, multimodal LLM evaluation, synthetic billiards, not aligned with continuous models or generation

Abstract: Current multimodal models handle static image recognition well, but intuitive physical reasoning remains a weakness. Predicting how objects will move and interact from a single image is still difficult for these systems. We present BilliardPhys-Bench, a benchmark for physical reasoning in synthetic billiards environments. Its procedural engine generates randomized scenarios with friction and elastic collisions. The benchmark tests three abilities: (1) predicting ball-to-ball collisions, (2) reasoning about wall bounces, and (3) estimating final ball positions after motion stops. We evaluate recent MLLMs from the GPT, Claude, Gemini, and Qwen families. Performance drops as simulation time increases and scene geometry grows more complex. We also observe a consistent failure mode we call "stasis bias": when the correct physical outcome is harder to infer, models tend to predict no interaction. These findings show where current MLLMs break down on visual dynamics and point toward the need for better physical inductive biases in multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [95] [GraphARC: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Graph-Based Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31031)
*Saku Peltonen, August Bøgh Rønberg, Andreas Plesner, Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GraphARC 是一个基于图结构的抽象推理基准，将 ARC 的少样本变换学习范式推广到图数据上，涵盖局部、全局和层次化图变换。评估显示现有语言模型在图属性问答上表现尚可，但在完整图变换任务上存在明显的"理解-执行鸿沟"，且在大规模实例上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抽象推理基准（如 ARC）局限于网格或文本格式，缺乏对图结构数据的系统性评测。图结构在现实世界中无处不在（如知识图谱、分子结构、社交网络），需要一个能够评估模型在图数据上进行关系推理和变换学习能力的基准。

Method: GraphARC 将 ARC 的少样本变换学习范式推广到图数据上。每个任务包含若干输入-输出图对，模型需从中推断变换规则并应用于新的测试图。基准覆盖局部（如节点/边添加删除）、全局（如图同构变换）和层次化（如子图合并/分解）三类图变换。支持跨不同图族和大小的规模化生成，便于系统评估泛化能力。

Result: 在多个 SOTA 语言模型上的评估显示：模型能回答关于图属性的问题，但在完整图变换任务上表现不佳，存在"理解-执行鸿沟"；在更大图实例上性能显著下降，暴露出扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: GraphARC 提供了一个结合节点分类、链接预测和图生成于一体的统一评测框架，揭示了当前语言模型在图结构抽象推理方面的局限性，为未来图基础模型的评估提供了有前景的测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 GraphARC，一个基于图结构的抽象推理基准，属于多模态/推理评测范畴。虽然其数据形式是图而非传统视觉/语言模态，但作为抽象推理基准，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）主题高度吻合，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: GraphARC is a new benchmark for graph-based abstract reasoning, but it is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Graph abstract reasoning benchmark, ARC generalization, comprehension-execution gap in LLMs, not aligned with user's priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Relational reasoning lies at the heart of intelligence, but existing benchmarks are typically confined to formats such as grids or text. We introduce GraphARC, a benchmark for abstract reasoning on graph-structured data. GraphARC generalizes the few-shot transformation learning paradigm of the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC). Each task requires inferring a transformation rule from a few input-output pairs and applying it to a new test graph, covering local, global, and hierarchical graph transformations. Unlike grid-based ARC, GraphARC instances can be generated at scale across diverse graph families and sizes, enabling systematic evaluation of generalization abilities. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models on GraphARC and observe clear limitations. Models can answer questions about graph properties but often fail to solve the full graph transformation task, revealing a comprehension-execution gap. Performance further degrades on larger instances, exposing scaling barriers. More broadly, by combining aspects of node classification, link prediction, and graph generation within a single framework, GraphARC provides a promising testbed for future graph foundation models.

</details>
