TL;DR: FAM-Bench 是一个多模态食物即药物（Food-as-Medicine）评测基准，包含 2500 个营养专家验证的实例，覆盖 13 种饮食相关健康状况，通过菜肴适宜性评估和比较分析两个任务，测试语言和视觉语言模型在健康约束下的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食物 AI 基准主要评估菜肴识别、食谱理解、营养估算或一般营养问答，缺乏对"特定食物是否适合特定健康状况"这一健康感知决策层的系统评测。

Method: 构建了包含 2500 个营养专家验证实例的 FAM-Bench 基准，覆盖 13 种饮食相关健康状况，设计两个互补任务：1）菜肴级适宜性评估（判断菜肴是否适合某健康状况）；2）比较性菜肴分析（对四个候选菜肴按条件适宜性排序）。两个任务均需整合食材证据、视觉制备线索和临床营养约束。

Result: 论文未在摘要中报告具体实验结果，但指出该基准为语言和视觉语言模型在健康感知推理方面提供了标准化测试平台。

Conclusion: FAM-Bench 填补了食物 AI 在健康感知决策层评测的空白，为评估模型在临床营养约束下的多模态推理能力提供了标准化基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向"食物即药物"场景的多模态评测基准 FAM-Bench，包含菜肴适宜性评估和比较分析两个任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Benchmark for food-as-medicine reasoning is niche and lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal benchmark, health-aware reasoning, food AI, condition-aware evaluation, no autoregressive or continuous model focus

Abstract: Food-as-Medicine requires models to reason beyond what a dish is or what nutrition it contains: they must decide whether a concrete food choice is appropriate for a specific health condition. Existing food AI benchmarks primarily evaluate dish recognition, recipe understanding, nutrient estimation, or general nutrition question answering, leaving this health-aware decision layer largely untested. We introduce FAM-Bench, a multi-modal Food-as-Medicine benchmark with 2500 nutrition-expert-verified instances across 13 diet-related health conditions. The benchmark contains two complementary tasks: dish-level suitability assessment, where models judge whether a dish is suitable for a condition from its image and ingredient list, and comparative dish analysis, where models rank four candidate dishes by condition-specific suitability. Both tasks require integrating ingredient evidence, visual preparation cues, and clinical nutrition constraints, providing a standardized testbed for grounded health-aware reasoning in language and vision-language models.

</details>


### [97] [MineExplorer: Evaluating Open-World Exploration of MLLM Agents in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30931)
*Tianjie Ju, Yueqing Sun, Zheng Wu, Wei Zhang, Yaqi Huo, Xi Su, Qi Gu, Xunliang Cai, Gongshen Liu, Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MineExplorer 是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型智能体在 Minecraft 开放世界中探索能力的基准。它通过筛选原子任务、构建 ReAct 风格的多跳任务，并采用多智能体合成流程生成可靠实例，发现开放世界探索对当前 MLLM 仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于游戏和具身智能的基准通常将交互压缩为短时任务，或与特定游戏机制纠缠，难以评估 MLLM 智能体在动态开放世界中的持续探索能力。

Method: 首先筛选出不依赖 Minecraft 特定知识的原子任务以反映通用开放世界推理；然后围绕 ReAct 风格能力框架将原子任务组合为隐式多跳任务；最后使用多智能体合成工作流联合设计任务图、沙盒场景和基于规则的里程碑评估器，生成可靠实例。

Result: 人类评估表明多智能体合成工作流比单智能体基线产生更可靠的实例。实验发现开放世界探索仍具挑战性：强模型能处理许多单跳任务，但在需要协调隐藏先决条件的长轨迹任务中性能急剧下降。任务难度与智能体完成度相关，更大模型或思维模式并不一致地带来更好性能。

Conclusion: MineExplorer 为评估 MLLM 智能体的开放世界探索能力提供了可靠基准，揭示了当前模型在长时序多跳任务中的局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MineExplorer 基准，用于评估 MLLM 智能体在 Minecraft 开放世界中的探索能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Evaluates MLLM agents in open-world exploration but lacks direct connection to your priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: MLLM agent benchmark, Minecraft open-world, multi-hop tasks, multi-agent synthesis, exploration capability evaluation

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong capabilities in perception, reasoning, and action generation. However, their ability to sustain exploration in dynamic open worlds remains unclear. Existing embodied and game-based benchmarks often compress interaction into short-horizon tasks or entangle success with domain-specific game mechanics. In this paper, we introduce MineExplorer benchmark for evaluating open-world exploration capabilities of MLLM agents in Minecraft. We first filter atomic tasks whose solutions rely heavily on Minecraft-specific knowledge to better reflect general open-world reasoning. Then we organize the benchmark around a ReAct-style capability formulation and compose atomic tasks into implicit multi-hop tasks. To further construct reliable instances, MineExplorer uses a multi-agent synthesis workflow that jointly designs task graphs, sandbox scenes, and rule-based milestone evaluators. Human evaluation shows that the multi-agent synthesis workflow produces significantly more reliable instances than a single-agent baseline. Experiments with advanced MLLM agents show that open-world exploration remains challenging, as strong models can handle many single-hop tasks but degrade sharply when hidden prerequisites must be coordinated over longer trajectories. Further analysis finds that task difficulty tracks agent completion, and larger models or thinking modes do not consistently translate into better performance. Code and dataset are available at .

</details>


### [98] [A Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31351)
*Yi Zhao, Siqi Wang, Zhe Hu, Yushi Li, Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VIABLE，首个面向视障辅助（VIA）场景的 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测基准，包含 30 万+ 判断样本和 12 种失效模式分类体系。系统评测 7 个 VLM 裁判后发现现有模型在有效性、公正性和稳定性上均不可靠，最强的 GPT-5.4 诊断准确率仅 52.6% 且自偏好率高达 94.2%。为此提出 VIA-Judge-Agent，通过视觉证据提取和分类引导工作流提升诊断准确率和下游 VIA 响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 AI 的视障辅助（VIA）任务因人工评估成本高昂而发展受限，VLM-as-a-Judge 范式虽在通用领域有研究，但在 VIA 场景中的可靠性尚未被系统验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估 VLM 裁判在 VIA 任务中的可信度。

Method: 构建 VIABLE 基准，包含 300K+ 判断样本，覆盖三个场景，提出 Effectiveness-Impartiality-Stability 评估框架和 12 种失效模式分类。系统评测 7 个不同规模的 VLM 裁判。提出 VIA-Judge-Agent，一种模型无关的推理时增强框架，通过视觉证据提取和分类引导工作流增强裁判能力。

Result: 现有 VLM 裁判在所有评估维度上均不可靠：最强模型 GPT-5.4 单失效诊断准确率仅 52.6%，自偏好率高达 94.2%；开源模型存在严重偏见且对抗脆弱。VIA-Judge-Agent 在诊断准确率和下游 VIA 响应质量上均取得正向提升。

Conclusion: 现有 VLM 裁判在视障辅助场景中远不可靠，需要专门的增强方法。VIA-Judge-Agent 展示了通过推理时增强提升裁判可靠性的可行路径，为 VIA 领域的自动评估提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VIABLE，首个面向视障辅助（VIA）场景的 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM-as-a-Judge for visually impaired assistance, which is a niche evaluation benchmark not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: VLM-as-a-Judge, visually impaired assistance, evaluation benchmark, failure taxonomy, model-agnostic agent

Abstract: AI-based Visually Impaired Assistance (VIA) remains challenging, largely due to the high cost of human evaluation. The VLM-as-a-Judge paradigm may offer a promising alternative, although it has mostly been studied in general domains. We therefore ask whether such judges can be trusted for VIA tasks. To investigate this question, we introduce VIABLE (Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation), the first benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge evaluation in VIA. VIABLE contains over 300K judgment samples across three scenarios and introduces an Effectiveness--Impartiality--Stability framework with a 12-mode failure taxonomy. Based on VIABLE, our systematic study of seven judges across different model scales shows that existing models are largely unreliable across all evaluation axes. The strongest judge, GPT-5.4, achieves only 52.6% single-failure diagnostic accuracy, yet exhibits the highest self-preference rate at 94.2%; while open-source judges are strongly biased and adversarially fragile. To address these issues, we propose VIA-Judge-Agent, a model-agnostic inference-time harness that augments judges with visual evidence extraction and a taxonomy-guided workflow. It enables positive improvements in diagnostic accuracy and downstream VIA responses more preferred by BLV users. Data and code are available at:

</details>


### [99] [ERGeoBench:A Comprehensive Benchmark for Embodied Reasoning and Geo-localization in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31251)
*Kaiwen Xue, Tao Wei, Guoxin Zhang, Zhonghong Ou, Kaoyan Lu, Yu Feng, Yifan Zhu, Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ERGeoBench是一个面向具身地理定位的多模态大模型诊断基准，包含2207个全球分布的街景全景图，在单视图、全景视图和具身视图三种渐进设置下评估模型的感知、空间推理、常识推理和地理定位能力。实验表明当前MLLM能推断高层地理语义，但在细粒度感知、度量定位和跨视图空间一致性方面仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在具身智能方面展现出潜力，但具身地理定位（embodied geo-localization）由于缺乏细粒度评测而未被充分探索。现有基准无法系统评估模型在主动获取观测（通过偏航、俯仰、缩放等操作）条件下的地理定位能力。

Method: 构建了ERGeoBench基准，包含2207个全球分布的街景全景图，设计了三种渐进评测设置：单视图（single-view）、全景视图（panorama-view）和具身视图（embodied-view，允许主动调整视角）。评测四个互补能力维度：基础感知、空间意识、常识推理和地理定位推理。对主流闭源和开源MLLM进行了系统评估。

Result: 当前MLLM能推断高层地理语义（如大洲/国家级别），但在细粒度感知操作、度量定位和跨视图空间一致性方面表现不佳。地理定位能力与其他能力维度（感知、空间推理、常识推理）高度相关，表明准确的地理定位依赖于综合能力而非孤立的视觉识别。

Conclusion: ERGeoBench为诊断和推进类人具身地理定位提供了统一框架，揭示了当前MLLM在该任务上的关键瓶颈，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了ERGeoBench，一个面向具身地理定位的多模态大模型诊断基准，属于多模态评测范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Benchmark for embodied geo-localization is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal understanding benchmark, geo-localization, street-view evaluation, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential as embodied agents, yet embodied geo-localization remains underexplored due to the lack of fine-grained evaluation. We introduce ERGeoBench, a diagnostic benchmark for vision-driven embodied geo-localization. ERGeoBench evaluates models under three progressive settings -- single-view, panorama-view, and embodied-view -- where agents may actively acquire observations through sequential changes in yaw, pitch, and zoom. The benchmark contains 2,207 globally distributed street-view panoramas and measures four complementary capabilities: foundational perception, spatial awareness, common sense reasoning, and geo-localization reasoning. Evaluations of leading proprietary and open-source MLLMs show that current models can infer high-level geographic semantics, but still struggle with fine-grained perceptual operations, metric localization, and spatial consistency across views. We further observe that geo-localization is strongly correlated with the other capability dimensions, suggesting that accurate localization depends on integrated perception, spatial reasoning, and commonsense inference rather than isolated visual recognition. Overall, ERGeoBench provides a unified framework for diagnosing and advancing human-like embodied geo-localization. Project Page:

</details>


### [100] [SVI-Bench: A Dynamic Microworld for Strategic Video Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31529)
*Yulu Pan, Han Yi, Seongsu Ha, Md Mohaiminul Islam, Benjamin Zhang, Lorenzo Torresani, Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SVI-Bench 是一个利用团队体育作为动态微世界的大规模基准，包含约 35K 小时广播视频、15M 标注动作等数据，设计了从动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟到智能体合成的四层九任务评测体系。实验发现最强模型在感知任务上约 73%，但在智能体任务上仅 5%，揭示了当前多模态模型在战略视频智能上的能力断层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准要么缺乏因果和战略问题的可验证真实标签（真实视频），要么牺牲了真实多智能体系统的复杂性（合成环境）。需要一个兼具真实世界复杂性和可验证性的基准来评测从感知到因果推理、模拟和战略规划的完整能力栈。

Method: 利用团队体育（篮球、足球、冰球）作为动态微世界，通过数据引擎将原始比赛数据转化为密集交叉引用的语料库，包含广播视频、动作标注、专家解说、比赛报告和统计数据。设计了四层九任务的渐进式评测体系：动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟和智能体合成。

Result: 强多模态和智能体基线模型在感知任务上表现尚可（细粒度动作 QA 约 73%），但在每个后续认知层级上急剧下降。智能体任务最难，最强模型在需要自主收集和整合 180 万片段语料证据的任务上仅达 5% 准确率。

Conclusion: SVI-Bench 揭示了当前多模态模型在战略视频智能上的能力断层，从感知到因果推理、模拟再到智能体决策，每个层级都存在显著性能下降，表明现有模型远未达到真正的视频智能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SVI-Bench，一个面向战略视频智能（从感知到因果推理、模拟到策略规划）的大规模多模态评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for video intelligence but lacks direct alignment with user's priority subtopics on continuous models and generation. Signals: Strategic video intelligence benchmark, multi-agent sports microworld, capability cliff from perception to agentic tasks, large-scale real-world data with verifiable ground truth, weak model performance on causal and strategic tasks

Abstract: True video intelligence demands more than recognizing what is visible: it requires reasoning about why events unfold, predicting what would change under different conditions, and deciding what to do next. We refer to this progression, from perception through causal reasoning and simulation to strategic planning, as Strategic Video Intelligence (SVI). No existing benchmark evaluates this capability stack: in-the-wild videos lack verifiable ground truth for causal and strategic questions, while synthetic environments sacrifice the complexity of real multi-agent systems. To bridge this gap, we introduce SVI-Bench, a large-scale benchmark that leverages team sports as a dynamic microworld, combining the complexity of real-world multi-agent interaction (10-22 agents making coordinated decisions under adversarial pressure) with the verifiability of explicit rules and definitive outcomes. SVI-Bench comprises approximately 35K hours of broadcast video, 15M annotated actions, 15K hours of expert commentary, 23K game reports, and 103K structured statistical records across basketball, soccer, and hockey, all constructed via a data engine that transforms raw game data into a dense, cross-referenced corpus. We organize evaluation into 9 tasks spanning a progressive four-pillar hierarchy: Dynamic Scene Understanding, Causal Reasoning, Strategic Simulation, and Agentic Synthesis. Evaluating strong multimodal and agentic baselines, we find a capability cliff: models perform competently on perceptual tasks, achieving approximately 73% on fine-grained action QA, but degrade sharply at each successive cognitive level. Agentic tasks prove hardest: the strongest model achieves only 5% accuracy when required to autonomously gather and integrate evidence across a corpus of 1.8M clips.

</details>


### [101] [nuReasoning: A Reasoning-Centric Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Tail Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31572)
*Zhiyu Huang, Johnson Liu, Rui Song, Zewei Zhou, Ruining Yang, Yun Zhang, Tianhui Cai, Hanyin Zhang, Mingxuan Gao, Valeria Xu, Jiali Chen, Yishan Shen, Yiluan Guo, Tony, Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: nuReasoning 是一个大规模真实世界自动驾驶推理数据集与基准，包含 20,000 个 20 秒长的视频片段，覆盖多城市多传感器数据，提供空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理的人工验证标注。实验表明，在该数据集上微调 VLM 能显著提升驾驶问答能力，且将推理监督融入 VLA 训练可提升规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准主要关注感知、预测或规划，缺乏对长尾场景中推理能力（常识推理、空间关系、交互推断、安全决策）的监督和评测。作者希望填补这一空白，推动自动驾驶系统向可解释、推理驱动的方向发展。

Method: 基于 nuScenes/nuPlan 数据，构建 20,000 个 20 秒长的驾驶片段，包含多相机图像、LiDAR、HD 地图、物体标注，并人工标注三类推理：空间推理（Spatial Reasoning）、决策推理（Decision Reasoning）和反事实推理（Counterfactual Reasoning）。支持推理评估和规划评估两种任务。

Result: 在 nuReasoning 上微调 VLM 显著提升了驾驶特定问答性能；将推理监督融入 VLA（Vision-Language-Action）训练中，即使推理时关闭文本推理输出，规划性能仍得到提升。

Conclusion: nuReasoning 为评估和改进长尾场景中鲁棒、可解释、推理驱动的自动驾驶系统提供了基础性数据集和基准，证明了推理监督对驾驶规划性能的正面影响。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 nuReasoning，一个面向长尾自动驾驶场景的推理数据集与评测基准，包含空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理标注，并支持推理评估与规划评估。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，是 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving reasoning, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Autonomous driving reasoning dataset, Long-tail scenario focus, VLM fine-tuning for driving QA, VLA planning improvement, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.

</details>


### [102] [SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31597)
*Olaf Dünkel, Basavaraj Sunagad, Haoran Wang, David T. Hoffmann, Christian Theobalt, Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SOCO是一个新的语义对象对应基准，包含100个类别、超过100万个对应点对，并引入对应类型分类学和关键点语言描述。实验发现视觉基础模型编码了强语义结构但跨类别对应能力弱，LVLM在文本引导定位上优于视觉参考跨图像匹配，且对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测下游密集任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型的评估缺乏对结构化对象理解（特别是部件级语义对应）的系统性评测，已有协议不一致且部件级监督有限。需要建立一个统一的、功能上有意义的基准来评估模型在跨实例和跨类别语义对应上的能力。

Method: 构建SOCO基准：在100个类别上提供功能上有意义的关键点标注，包含超过100万对应点对；引入对应类型分类学（如跨实例、跨类别对应）；为关键点提供语言描述以支持LVLM评估；系统评测多种视觉基础模型和LVLM的语义对应能力。

Result: (i) 视觉基础骨干网络编码了强语义结构，但跨相关类别传递对应关系能力差，仅部分捕获对象部件位置；(ii) LVLM在文本提示的部件定位上强于视觉参考的跨图像匹配，暴露了语言定位与细粒度视觉对应之间的差距；(iii) 对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测下游密集任务（分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计、3D检测）的表现。

Conclusion: SOCO可作为评估视觉和多模态基础模型中结构化、部件级表征质量的基准，揭示了当前模型在细粒度语义对应方面的局限性，并为未来研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了SOCO基准，用于评估视觉基础模型和视觉语言模型在语义对象对应（semantic object correspondence）方面的细粒度部件级理解能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic 'multimodal_benchmark'。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for vision model evaluation but outside user's priority subtopics of continuous models, generation, and world models. Signals: Semantic correspondence benchmark, vision foundation model evaluation, LVLM part localization gap, dense task performance predictor, no generation or continuous model focus

Abstract: Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [103] [BlueFin: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Financial Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30907)
*Srivatsa Kundurthy, Clara Na, Colton Moraine, Anoushka Mohta, Case Winter, George Fang, John Ling, Emma Strubell, Zach Kirshner*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.SE

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BlueFin是一个面向金融电子表格的LLM智能体评测基准，包含131个复杂任务和3225个细粒度评分标准，由专家人工标注验证。前沿LLM在该基准上表现不佳（平均得分低于50%），尤其在动态正确性方面存在明显弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管电子表格软件拥有数亿用户（远超专业开发者数量），但针对LLM在电子表格领域能力的探索和评测资源却非常有限，尤其是缺乏反映真实金融职业场景的复杂任务基准。

Method: 构建了包含131个金融电子表格任务的基准数据集，涵盖合成、操作和理解三类任务；设计了3225个细粒度评分标准，由专家人工验证；采用LM judge自动评估，并与专家共识达到高度一致（α=0.826）。

Result: 前沿LLM在BlueFin上平均得分低于50%，在动态正确性方面表现尤为薄弱；LM judge与专家共识的宏F1分数达到0.839。

Conclusion: BlueFin揭示了当前LLM在金融电子表格复杂任务上的显著不足，为未来研究提供了高质量的评测框架和基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向金融电子表格的LLM智能体评测基准BlueFin，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然基准聚焦于电子表格（结构化数据），但评测LLM智能体的理解、操作与综合能力，与多模态评测基准的范畴一致。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial spreadsheets, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes negative keywords. Signals: financial domain, spreadsheet benchmark, LLM agents, low model performance, expert-validated rubric

Abstract: We present BlueFin, a benchmark that tasks large language model (LLM) agents with synthesis, manipulation, and comprehension tasks over spreadsheet workbooks in the professional finance domain. Though estimates of the global population of paying users of spreadsheet software range in the hundreds of millions -- an order of magnitude more than the estimated global population of professional developers -- comparatively fewer resources have been devoted to exploring and expanding LLM capabilities in the spreadsheet domain, with fewer still dedicated to mirroring real occupational tasks encountered by those in professional finance roles. In response, we curate a set of 131 challenging, complex tasks with real-world relevance in the domain, containing 3,225 granular rubric criteria; notably, our rubric criteria and LM judge evaluations are validated by a team of expert human annotators, resulting in high-quality, granular evaluations of complex tasks that are difficult to verify programmatically but can be reliably evaluated by an LM judge agent. Our judge achieves parity with expert consensus ($\alpha=0.826$) with a macro-F1 score of 0.839. Frontier LLMs demonstrate poor performance on the challenging benchmark, with the strongest LLMs achieving less than 50\% average scores across tasks -- models exhibit particular weaknesses in dynamic correctness. Our contributions include a dataset of examples across three categories of spreadsheet tasks, an open source harness and agentic evaluation framework, and a characterization of existing frontier models' performance on our benchmark.

</details>

### [104] [VisionPulse: Dynamic Visual Sparsity for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31457)
*Hengbo Xu, Shengjie Jin, Yanbiao Ma, Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (68.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisionPulse 提出了一种推理时逐步视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过轻量级注意力质量估计每步保留预算，仅保留 5% 的视觉 token，同时将推理轨迹缩短 11.2%，精度几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常在预填充阶段一次性剪枝视觉 token，假设推理过程中所需的视觉证据是静态的。然而本文发现视觉证据具有强烈的步骤依赖性：每个解码步骤仅需少量关键视觉 token，且关键集随推理过程动态变化。此外，冗余视觉上下文会引导模型关注与查询无关的区域，导致推理轨迹变长。因此需要一种推理时动态剪枝方法。

Method: VisionPulse 框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级视觉注意力质量（visual attention mass）计算，用于估计每步的 token 保留预算——利用该质量与 LMM 有效视觉 token 使用率之间的强正相关性；(2) 在该预算下仅保留最关键的视觉 token，实现逐步稀疏化。通过强制推理时视觉稀疏性，过滤冗余视觉上下文同时保留相关证据，自然缩短推理轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，VisionPulse 每步仅保留 5% 的视觉 token，推理轨迹缩短 11.2%，同时保持精度几乎不变。

Conclusion: VisionPulse 验证了推理过程中视觉证据的动态性，并提出了一种高效的逐步剪枝方案，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持模型性能，为多模态大模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VisionPulse，一种在推理过程中动态稀疏化视觉 token 的方法，旨在降低多模态大模型（LMM）的推理开销。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，具体涉及 token 压缩与稀疏推理。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient multimodal reasoning via dynamic visual sparsity, aligning with user's priority on model efficiency and continuous multimodal models. Signals: efficient inference, visual token pruning, multimodal reasoning, step-wise sparsity, reasoning trace shortening

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large multimodal models (LMMs), inference-time overhead has become a key bottleneck for real-world deployment. Existing methods typically prune visual tokens at prefill, assuming the required visual evidence remains static during reasoning. However, we empirically show that visual evidence is strongly step-dependent: only a sparse subset of visual tokens is critical at each decoding step, and the critical set evolves across reasoning. Furthermore, we identify a coupled bottleneck where redundant visual context can steer the model toward query-irrelevant regions, lengthening the reasoning trace. Guided by these insights, we propose VisionPulse, a step-wise visual token pruning framework during reasoning. VisionPulse computes a lightweight visual attention mass to estimate the step-wise retention budget by exploiting its strong positive correlation with LMMs' effective visual token usage and retain only the most critical tokens under this budget. By enforcing visual sparsity during reasoning, VisionPulse filters redundant visual context while preserving relevant visual evidence, shortening reasoning traces naturally. Extensive experiments show that VisionPulse only retains 5% of visual tokens per step with reasoning traces shortened by 11.2%, while keeping accuracy almost unchanged.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-spatial_3d_reasoning'></div>

### [105] [VLM-GLoc: Vision-Language Model Enhanced Monte Carlo Localization for Robust Semantic Global Localization in Cluttered Quasi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30506)
*Shivendra Agrawal, Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLM-GLoc 提出一种层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位方法，利用开放词汇 VLM 作为统一语义观测前端，通过逆语义提议机制和文本到地图检索实现杂乱准静态环境中的鲁棒全局定位，在杂货店和实验室场景中分别达到 70% 和 74% 的全局定位成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何混淆（如平行货架）且准静态的室内环境（杂货店、办公室、实验室）中，传统基于几何特征或专用视觉管线的全局定位方法难以处理长尾语义分布和瞬态视觉杂乱，亟需一种更鲁棒的语义全局定位方法。

Method: 提出 VLM-GLoc，一种层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位（MCL）方法。核心包括：(1) 利用开放词汇 VLM 作为统一语义观测前端，提取高判别性文本特征；(2) 隐式质量过滤模糊或动态物体；(3) 持久性推理用于目标数据增强；(4) 逆语义提议机制通过文本到地图检索播种粒子。

Result: 在两个真实环境（3500 平方英尺杂货店 + 手机平台、3700 平方英尺实验室 + 四足机器人）上，VLM-GLoc 分别达到 70% 和 74% 的全局定位成功率，显著优于传统纯几何方法和专用视觉基线。

Conclusion: VLM 作为统一语义观测前端能有效提升机器人在杂乱准静态环境中的全局定位鲁棒性，逆语义提议机制和持久性推理是关键创新点，未来可扩展到更多动态场景。

Classification reason: 该论文利用 VLM 增强机器人全局定位，属于空间推理与具身定位的交叉，与 canonical subtopic "空间与 3D 多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics localization with VLM, not on core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: VLM for localization, semantic MCL, quasi-static environments, robotics application, not generative or continuous token

Abstract: Global localization in geometrically aliased, quasi-static environments such as grocery stores, offices, schools, and hospitals poses a significant challenge for mobile robots. Grocery stores with parallel aisles and a long tailed distribution of products, as well as offices and labs with repetitive furniture such as chairs, desks, monitors, and doors, exemplify common indoor environments that present geometric and even semantic ambiguity. Traditional approaches rely either on distinct geometric features or on domain-specific vision pipelines that struggle with long-tail semantic distributions and transient visual clutter. We present VLM-GLoc, a method for hierarchical semantic Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) that leverages open-vocabulary Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified semantic observation front-end. We hypothesize a three-fold benefit from VLMs: (1) extracting highly discriminative rich text features, (2) implicit quality filtering of blurry or dynamic objects, and (3) permanence reasoning for targeted data augmentation. We introduce an inverse semantic proposal mechanism that seeds particles via text-to-map retrieval. Evaluated across two real-world environments with different characteristics and two different platforms: a 3,500 sq. ft. grocery store with a cellphone and a 3,700 sq. ft. lab space with a quadruped, VLM-GLoc achieves 70% and 74% global localization success respectively, substantially outperforming traditional geometry-only and domain-specific baselines.

</details>


### [106] [VLM3: Vision Language Models Are Native 3D Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30561)
*Zhipeng Cai, Zhuang Liu, Yunyang Xiong, Zechun Liu, Vikas Chandra, Yangyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (69.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLM3 提出一种极简方法，通过焦距统一、文本像素参考和数据混合缩放，使标准 VLM 无需架构修改、大模型或复杂损失函数，即可在深度估计、像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级 3D 理解等任务上达到专家视觉模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在语义理解上表现优异，但 3D 理解仍依赖复杂的专家视觉模型和任务特定设计。作者希望证明 VLM 本身是天然的 3D 学习者，只需简单改造即可胜任多种 3D 任务，从而开启简单可扩展的 3D 学习新范式。

Method: VLM3 的核心方法包含三个关键组件：(1) 焦距统一（Focal Length Unification）——将不同焦距的图像归一化到统一视角；(2) 文本像素参考（Text-based Pixel Reference）——用文本 token 表示像素坐标，使 VLM 能通过文本输出进行像素级预测；(3) 数据混合与缩放（Data Mixture and Scaling）——混合多种 3D 任务数据并扩大规模。无需改变模型架构、使用大模型、复杂数据增强或回归损失。

Result: VLM3 将 VLM 深度估计精度从 0.84 提升至 0.9（SILog 指标），并在像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级 3D 理解等任务上达到与专家视觉模型相当的精度，同时保持标准架构和基于文本的训练方式。

Conclusion: VLM3 证明了标准 VLM 通过简单设计即可成为强大的 3D 学习者，无需复杂的架构或损失函数。这为 3D 视觉任务提供了一种新的简单可扩展范式，有望推动 VLM 在 3D 理解领域的广泛应用。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何让标准 VLM 通过简单方法（焦距统一、文本像素参考、数据混合与缩放）掌握深度估计、像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级 3D 理解等 3D 任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与 3D 多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）范畴。

Importance reason: Shows standard VLMs can natively learn 3D tasks without complex modifications, aligning with user's priority on unified multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: unified multimodal model, 3D understanding, scalable method, standard VLM architecture, depth estimation

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) enable a unified model to solve various vision tasks through prompting. They have shown promising performance in semantic understanding. However, 3D understanding still largely relies on expert vision models with complex task-specific designs. The key argument this work wants to make is that VLMs are native 3D learners. Our in-depth large scale study shows that 1) focal length unification, 2) text-based pixel reference and 3) data mixture and scaling, are all you need for effective 3D learning. Model architecture changes, large models, heavy data augmentations, and complex losses including the regression formulation, many of which form the foundation of expert vision models, are actually not necessary conditions. As a result, we propose VLM3, a scalable method with the simplest design that enables standard VLMs to master diverse 3D tasks. VLM3 not only advances the VLM depth estimation accuracy by a large margin (0.84 -> 0.9), but also enables diverse 3D tasks such as pixel correspondence, camera pose estimation and object-level 3D understanding, matching expert vision model accuracy while maintaining standard architectures and text-based training. We believe VLM3 opens up a new paradigm for simple and scalable 3D learning.

</details>


### [107] [Multi-Turn Multi-Agent Dialogue for Collaborative Reconstruction Improves VLM Performance on Spatial Reasoning, But Only Barely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31387)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti, Sherzod Hakimov, David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (51.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过一个协作式结构搭建任务，系统评估了多种VLM在空间推理、视觉定位和语言引导交互方面的能力。研究发现，VLM在视觉空间推理上仍存在显著困难，详细的文本描述比视觉输入更有效，分解式图像表示能提升性能，但整体表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类协作任务中需要依赖视觉输入理解物体和空间布局，并通过语言进行沟通。尽管VLM在视觉解释、问答和指令跟随方面取得了进展，但它们在需要空间推理的协作对话任务中的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 设计了一个协作式结构搭建任务框架，VLM通过多轮多智能体对话来从视觉和文本输入中重建目标结构。评估了多种开源和闭源VLM，比较了不同交互设置、输入模态和图像表示方式对重建成功率的影响。

Result: VLM在视觉空间推理上表现困难；详细的文本表示比视觉输入带来更高的重建成功率；分解式图像表示（如分块图像）能提升性能；但整体上VLM在视觉空间定位和基于定位的指令生成方面仍存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在协作式空间推理任务中的视觉空间定位和指令生成能力有限，提示需要进一步改进VLM的空间推理能力，尤其是在视觉表征的理解和利用方面。

Classification reason: 论文研究VLM在协作式空间推理任务中的表现，涉及视觉空间理解、语言引导交互和动作生成，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与3D多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）规范子主题。同时与具身世界模型（embodied_world_model）相关，因为任务涉及机器人协作场景中的视觉空间推理与动作生成。

Importance reason: Spatial reasoning in VLMs is relevant but the paper's negative findings and narrow task scope limit its impact for your priorities. Signals: spatial reasoning evaluation, VLM limitations, collaborative dialogue, decomposed image representation, negative result

Abstract: Robots operating in diverse environments rely on visual input to interpret objects and spatial layouts. In human-collaborative tasks, they are expected to communicate this understanding through language. Vision-language models (VLMs) support robotic tasks involving visual interpretation, question answering, and instruction following, but their capabilities in collaborative dialogue tasks requiring spatial reasoning remain underexplored. We study this gap through a collaborative structure-building task that combines visual interpretation, grounding, language-guided interaction, and action generation. We develop a framework in which VLMs use dialogue to reconstruct a target structure from visual and textual inputs. We evaluate open-weight and closed VLMs across interaction settings, input modalities, and image representations. Results show that spatial reasoning over visual representations remains difficult for the evaluated VLMs. Detailed text representations of the target yield higher reconstruction success across modality conditions, while decomposed image representations improve performance. These findings reveal limits in visual spatial grounding and grounded instruction generation for collaborative VLM agents.

</details>


### [108] [SpatialAct: Probing Spatial Reasoning-to-Action Capabilities of VLM Agents in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31148)
*Tianhui Liu, Jie Feng, Zhiheng Zheng, Shengyuan Wang, Yiming Guo, Yanxin Xi, Hangyu Fan, Yong Li, Pan Hui*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (66.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpatialAct 是一个基于模拟器的基准，用于探测 VLM 智能体在 3D 场景中的动作条件空间推理能力。实验发现当前 VLM 在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮交互反馈中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠动作，存在明显的推理到动作的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松感知空间布局、形成认知表征、推理空间关系并将其转化为日常 3D 环境中的动作。虽然当前 VLM 在观察条件下的空间感知和推理任务上表现不错，但它们能否构建连贯的空间理解、据此采取行动并通过多轮反馈优化动作，仍不清楚。本文旨在系统探测这一能力。

Method: 提出 SpatialAct 基准，包含三个层次的任务设置：(1) 最具挑战性的多轮交互式精炼（Multi-turn Interactive Refinement）；(2) 分解后的单步错误检测与修复（Single-step Error Detection and Fix）；(3) 五种基础空间能力诊断任务。使用模拟器生成 3D 场景，评估 VLM 智能体在动作条件空间推理中的表现。

Result: 实验揭示了清晰的推理到动作鸿沟：当前 VLM 在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮反馈中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠动作，显著落后于人类表现。这表明即使抽象掉底层控制，VLM 智能体仍缺乏在动作引起的环境变化下进行鲁棒空间状态跟踪的能力。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 智能体在 3D 场景中的动作条件空间推理存在根本性不足，尤其是在多轮交互场景中。SpatialAct 基准为未来研究提供了诊断工具，有助于推动 VLM 从感知推理到实际动作执行的能力提升。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 在 3D 场景中的空间推理到动作的能力，属于空间与 3D 多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。同时涉及具身智能环境中的交互式推理，与 world_model 方向下的 embodied_world_model 也有一定关联。

Importance reason: Probes a critical reasoning-to-action gap in VLM agents for 3D scenes, relevant to multimodal understanding and world model building. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning benchmark, reasoning-to-action gap, multi-turn interactive refinement, 3D scene understanding, simulator-grounded evaluation

Abstract: Humans can effortlessly perceive spatial layouts, form cognitive representations, reason about spatial relations, and translate such reasoning into actions in everyday 3D environments. Although recent vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance on observation-conditioned spatial perception and reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they can build coherent spatial understanding, act upon it, and refine their actions through multi-turn feedback. To study this problem, we introduce \textbf{SpatialAct}, a simulator-grounded benchmark for probing \textit{action-conditioned spatial reasoning} in 3D scenes. Starting from the most challenging setting, Multi-turn Interactive Refinement, we further design its decomposed counterpart, Single-step Error Detection and Fix, together with five fundamental spatial ability tasks to diagnose the underlying causes of model failures. Experiments reveal a clear reasoning-to-action gap: current VLMs can perform well on isolated spatial reasoning tasks, but struggle to maintain coherent spatial beliefs and produce reliable actions during multi-turn feedback, substantially underperforming humans. These results suggest that current VLM agents still lack robust spatial state tracking under action-induced environment changes, even when low-level control is abstracted away.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-unified_multimodal_model'></div>

## 统一多模态模型 [[Back]](#toc)

理解与生成一体化、统一 token 表征、Any-to-Any 多模态模型。

### [109] [Lumos-Nexus: Efficient Frequency Bridging with Homogeneous Latent Space for Video Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31603)
*Jiazheng Xing, Hangjie Yuan, Lingling Cai, Xinyu Liu, Yujie Wei, Fei Du, Hai Ci, Tao Feng, Jiasheng Tang, Weihua Chen, Fan Wang, Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Lumos-Nexus 提出了一种训练高效的视频统一模型框架，采用两阶段设计：训练时仅对齐轻量生成器与理解模块，推理时通过统一渐进频率桥接（UPFB）在共享潜空间中逐步切换到高容量预训练生成器，实现粗到细的高保真视频生成。同时提出 VR-Bench 基准评估推理驱动视频生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 connector 的视频统一模型在指令引导的视频合成方面表现良好，但将大型高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中计算成本过高，限制了可实现的视觉质量。因此需要一种训练高效的框架，既能发展强大的推理驱动生成能力，又能显著提升视觉保真度。

Method: Lumos-Nexus 采用两阶段设计：(1) 训练阶段，仅将轻量生成器与理解模块对齐，学习接收推理驱动的语义控制；(2) 推理阶段，引入统一渐进频率桥接（UPFB），在共享潜空间中逐步将生成任务移交给高容量预训练生成器，实现粗到细的细化。同时构建 VR-Bench 基准用于评估推理驱动视频生成能力。

Result: 在 VBench 上，Lumos-Nexus 在视觉真实感和时序连贯性方面取得了显著提升；在 VR-Bench 上展示了强大的基于推理的生成性能。

Conclusion: Lumos-Nexus 通过训练高效的统一视频生成框架，在不牺牲推理质量的前提下实现了高保真视频生成，为推理驱动的视频生成提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Lumos-Nexus，一个基于 connector 的视频统一模型框架，将理解模块与轻量生成器对齐训练，推理时通过渐进频率桥接（UPFB）切换到高保真预训练生成器，实现推理驱动的视频生成。这属于理解与生成一体化（unified multimodal model）的典型工作，因此映射到该 canonical subtopic。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-efficient video unified model with frequency bridging in shared latent space, directly advancing unified multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: unified multimodal model, efficient inference, video generation, frequency bridging, shared latent space

Abstract: Connector-based video unified models have demonstrated strong capability in instruction-grounded video synthesis, but integrating a large high-fidelity generator into the unified training loop is computationally prohibitive, limiting achievable visual quality. We therefore propose Lumos-Nexus, a training-efficient unified video generation framework that facilitates the development of strong reasoning-driven generation capabilities while significantly enhancing visual fidelity. Lumos-Nexus adopts a two-stage design: 1) During training, only a lightweight generator is aligned with the understanding block to learn to take in reasoning-driven semantic control. 2) During inference, we introduce Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging (UPFB) to progressively hand off generation to a high-capacity pretrained generator in the shared latent space, enabling coarse-to-fine refinement and producing high-fidelity videos without compromising reasoning quality. To fill the gap in reasoning-driven video generation benchmarks, we introduce VR-Bench, which assesses a model's capability to translate inferred intent into coherent and semantically aligned video content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Lumos-Nexus achieves substantial gains in visual realism and temporal coherence on VBench, while exhibiting strong reasoning-based generative performance on VR-Bench. Code and models are available at .

</details>


### [110] [Representation Forcing for Bottleneck-Free Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31604)
*Yuqing Wang, Zhijie Lin, Ceyuan Yang, Yang Zhao, Fei Xiao, Hao He, Qi Zhao, Zihan Ding, Fuyun Wang, Shuai Wang, Youliang Zhang, Haoqi Fan, Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.67) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 技术，通过让解码器自回归预测视觉表征作为中间 token，再在相同骨干网络中进行像素扩散，消除了统一多模态模型对独立预训练 VAE 的依赖，实现了无瓶颈的端到端理解与生成一体化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMM）仍依赖冻结的、单独预训练的 VAE 进行图像生成，这引入了结构瓶颈。直接移除 VAE 会导致生成质量下降，因为模型需要同时从原始像素中学习高层结构和低层细节。作者希望消除这一瓶颈，实现真正的端到端统一模型。

Method: 提出 Representation Forcing (RF)：在解码过程中，模型先自回归地预测视觉表征（如 DINOv2 特征）作为中间 token，这些 token 保留在上下文中，然后通过同一骨干网络中的扩散过程生成像素。RF 将表征从感知输出转变为生成目标，使模型原生具备表征预测能力，无需外部生成潜空间。

Result: 在图像生成上，像素空间模型搭配 RF 达到了与基于 VAE 的最先进统一模型相当的性能。在图像理解上，像素空间 RF 模型通常优于其基于 VAE 的变体。RF 同时提升了理解与生成能力。

Conclusion: Representation Forcing 是迈向无瓶颈、端到端统一多模态模型的有效步骤，证明了在像素空间中无需外部 VAE 即可实现高质量的理解与生成统一。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 方法，旨在消除统一多模态模型（UMM）中依赖外部 VAE 的结构瓶颈，实现像素空间中的理解与生成一体化。这直接对应 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model），同时涉及图像生成（multimodal_generation方向）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses bottleneck-free unified multimodal models, a top priority subtopic, with a novel technique that eliminates VAE dependency. Signals: unified multimodal model, bottleneck-free, representation forcing, autoregressive visual tokens, pixel-space generation

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to handle perception and generation in a single model. Yet existing UMMs still rely on a frozen, separately pretrained VAE for image generation, imposing a structural bottleneck. Naively removing it introduces a quality gap, as the model must learn both high-level structure and low-level details from raw pixels. In this paper, we propose Representation Forcing (RF), a technique that closes this gap by making representation prediction a native capability of the model. Concretely, RF forces the decoder to autoregressively predict visual representations as intermediate tokens before pixels; these tokens then stay in context to guide pixel diffusion within the same backbone. By turning representations from perception outputs into generation targets, RF eliminates the need for any external generative latent space. We find that RF benefits both understanding and generation. On image generation, our pixel-space model with RF matches state-of-the-art VAE-based unified models. On image understanding, pixel-space RF generally outperforms its VAE-based variant. Together, these results offer an effective step toward end-to-end, bottleneck-free UMMs.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)
